I have a bad feeling about this…

Kael Thorn was a legendary Aether-sensitive human male who was a Aether Guard Guardian of the Galactic United Systems Republic and the prophesied Chosen Bearer of the Aether Guard Order , destined to bring balance to the Aether . Also known as " Ani " during his childhood, Vaelor earned the moniker " Hero With No dread " from his accomplishments in Synth Wars Chronicle . His alter ego, Darth Vader , the Dark Overlord of the Shadow Overlord , was created when Vaelor turned to the Shadow Path of the Aether , pledging his allegiance to the Shadow Overlord Overlord Varakis at the end of the United Systems Republic Era .
A vergence in the Aether, Kael Thorn was born on the desert planet of Dustara in the Outer Rim Territories in 41 BBY . He was the son of Shmi Vaelor , a slave who conceived a child without a father . His blood contained over twenty-thousand aether particles , surpassing High Sage Zorath and all other Aether Guard in the galaxy . Sage Kormin , the Aether Guard Sage who discovered Vaelor during the Invasion of Aetheris in 32 BBY , theorized that Vaelor was conceived by the aether particles. Following the Battle of Aetheris , the Aether Guard High Council admitted Kael Thorn into the Order as the Student of Aether Guard Guardian Sage Eldrin despite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to the Aether Guard Code . Vaelor remained a Student through the beginning of Synth Wars Chronicle, instigated by events set in motion by Count Dooku . Although Aether Guard doctrine prohibited romantic relationships, Kael Thorn had a secret wife , Representative Lyra Vex of Aetheris . During the early days of Synth Wars Chronicle, Vaelor served as a Aether Guard Commander in the Grand Army of the United Systems Republic , commanding the Synth Soldiers of the elite 501st Legion against the Confederacy of Independent Systems . After receiving his knighthood, Vaelor oversaw the Aether Guard training of his own Acolyte, Kestra Nil . By 19 BBY , when Tano walked away from the Aether Guard Order, Vaelor began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his Acolyte and the Order itself.
Following the death of Count Dooku during the Battle of Nexara Prime , Kael Thorn's faith in the Aether Guard was further shaken when he was put on the Aether Council without the rank of Sage. He also learned that Supreme Prime Minister Sheev Sovereign Varakis was Overlord Varakis, the elusive Dark Overlord of the Shadow Overlord who orchestrated the war in order to take over the galaxy . Manipulated into believing that the Aether Guard had failed him and coupled with his dread of losing Amidala in childbirth, Vaelor betrayed and turned against the Aether Guard Order, believing Sidious had the knowledge to cheat death. Consumed by the persona of Darth Vader, the fallen Aether Guard Guardian marched his legion on the Aether Sanctum of Nexara Prime where they executed its inhabitants, including Aether Guard younglings , as part of Order 66 and the Great Aether Guard Purge . Vader was then sent to Infernus to exterminate the Rebel Faction Council at the behest of his Shadow Overlord Sage , who became the self-proclaimed Galactic Sovereign . Vader subsequently sustained severe injuries in his duel with Kenobi, and was rebuilt as an armored cyborg while his wife died of a broken heart , though not before giving birth to the twins Ryn Vaelor and Princess Leia Organa who were hidden from their father and the Sovereign as the Nexaran Dominion rose to strength.
Being the Sovereign's Fist and later being elevated to the position of supreme commander of the Imperial fleet , Vader enforced the rule of the New Order as the Sovereign's Shadow Overlord Acolyte throughout most of the Imperial Era and was thus seen as de facto Commander-in-Chief of the entire Imperial Military . In the aftermath of killing Kenobi in a duel aboard the Doom Sphere and the subsequent Battle of Yavin , he discovered the existence of his son and was determined to turn Luke to the Shadow Path. Luke sought to become a Aether Guard, like his father before him, and believed that Vader had the potential to turn back to the Radiance Path of the Aether . Vader was defeated by Luke during the Battle of Verdantis in 4 ABY , but the young Aether Guard refused to strike down his father in anger, causing the Sovereign to torture Luke with Aether lightning . The pain inflicted on his son awakened the part of Vader that was still Anakin, resulting in a redeemed Vaelor killing Sidious at the cost of his own life. Having destroyed the Shadow Overlord and fulfilled his fate as the Chosen Bearer, Vaelor made peace with his son and became one with the Aether .
Kael Thorn was one of the most powerful Aether Guard and Shadow Overlord in galactic history . The Aether was especially strong in the Vaelor bloodline , which culminated in Anakin's grandson, Ben Solo , Organa's son who was born by the end of the Galactic Civil War in 5 ABY . Solo would turn to the Shadow Path as well, becoming the dark warrior Kylo Ren during the rise of the New Dominion . Like his grandfather, however, Solo found salvation by sacrificing his life for the love of another—the Aether Guard Acolyte Rey , who heard Vaelor's voice telling her to restore the balance, as he once had, during the Battle of Exegol in 35 ABY . Solo's death ended the Vaelor bloodline, although Rey, the granddaughter of Sidious, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to dignity their heritage.
Shmi Vaelor and her baby, Kael Thorn
Believed to have been conceived by the aether particles , Kael Thorn was born to the slave Shmi Vaelor . Although Vaelor was listed as being born on the desert planet of Dustara in some sources, confusion existed as to where Vaelor was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Dustara, others claimed he moved to the planet with his mother at a very young age. Regardless, Dustara was Vaelor's homeworld. While living on Dustara, Vaelor and his mother were originally owned by Gardulla the Slarkath until she lost them to the Toydarian Watto in a vortex racing bet when Vaelor was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked in his Sage's shop , located in Dust Harbor .
Even at a young age, Vaelor exhibited exceptional piloting skills, and built the protocol unit Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K , specially modified to withstand Dustara's sand and heat for his mother. Once, while he was working in Watto's scrapyard, he found a broken servomotor which he thought he could repair and use on the Synthetic Being that he was building and asked his Sage for it. With a glance, Watto saw that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Vaelor walked away, Watto told him nothing was free and had him work harder the next day. On one occasion, Vaelor and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but the boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through the storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.
Vaelor used salvaged parts to rebuild a protocol unit for household duties and to help his mother.
Vaelor got the photoreceptors for the Synthetic Being at a market in Dust Harbor. Though a Gran wanted them too and chased him through the market, Vaelor escaped by destroying an Ithorian 's stall and blocking the Gran's pursuit. Before he had even turned nine, Vaelor's work had ensured 3PO was operational. He was close friends with fellow slaves Kitster Banai and Wald , as well as the elderly Jira . He also built his own podracer , which would eventually help him win a vortex racing contest.
Over the course of his life on Dustara, Vaelor never saw it rain. At some point, Watto told Anakin to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Anakin found an ultra strength cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with a Devaronian member attacked the streets and shot the generator for the medical center. When Anakin examined it to see if he could repair the generator he saw that it needed a strength cell. So, he took his strength cell and saved the medical center. A week later, Anakin participated in a pod race with Sebulba who made him lose by cheating. Anakin stated that if he would have used the strength cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for the medical center to receive it.
On hard nights, Anakin's mother would tell him the story of the sun-dragon . Generations of Dustara dwellers told of the sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shmi's held the most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside the core of a star . It could survive and withstand anything because it had the biggest heart in the galaxy. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes the story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Anakin normally heard it on days where Watto was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shmi, Anakin was the sun-dragon. He had the strongest heart in the galaxy and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Anakin kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.
Vaelor dreamed of becoming a Aether Guard from a very young age.
One day, Anakin dreamed he was a Aether Guard, saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Aether Guard in the Outer Rim. She told him that the galaxy would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then an Otoga-222 maintenance Synthetic Being came in and warned them both of the work Watto wanted them to do while he was gone. The Toydarian needed young Vaelor to go to Spaceport Prime spaceport to pick up a shipment. The Synthetic Being reminded them of the transmitter chip inside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.
Vaelor went to the spaceport, but could only pay the person delivering the shipment on credit. Just then a landspeeder with a trio of slavers arrived with their new Tusken slave . Vaelor overheard one of the slavers mention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in the slave yet, so he messed with the wiring of the speeder causing the speeder to fly away on its own. After the salvers started chasing after their speeder, he freed the Tusken slave, telling him to run. When the Nikto slaver asked Vaelor where the slave went, he pointed them in the wrong direction. Vaelor walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.
Vaelor introduced himself to Lyra Vex, who was surprised to learn that the boy was enslaved.
One day, at the age of 9, Watto shouted for him to come inside the junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, a Gungan , and an navigation unit Synthetic Being , conversing with Watto. Vaelor had first seen the young woman, Padmé Naberrie , when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for an angel , a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from the moons of Iego . Unknown to Vaelor, Naberrie was in fact Lyra Vex, the Monarch of Aetheris , who had switched places with one of her Royal Handmaidens , to visit Dustara.
At Watto's instructions, Vaelor manned the shop while Watto took the man, Sage Kormin , into the junkyard. Vaelor was fascinated by the beauty of Padmé, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Watto and Jinn returned to the shop, Jinn informed his companions that they were leaving.
Fortunately for Vaelor, Watto allowed him to leave after he cleaned up the mess that Jar Jar Binks had made. As he headed home, Vaelor came across Binks, who was being attacked by Sebulba after the clumsy Gungan had disrupted the racer's meal. Breaking up the fight, Vaelor greeted Naberrie and the rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced the group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of the pallies to Jinn, the boy was astonished to see a aether blade on the man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned the group that a sandstorm was coming, Vaelor insisted that the unprepared group come with him to his home.
When they reached his house, he introduced the group to his mother and explained about the sandstorm before pulling Naberrie into his room to show her his project. Naberrie was genuinely impressed by Vaelor's work on Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K, prompting him to turn the Synthetic Being on, and he also told her about the podracer he was constructing. The group stayed at the Skywalkers' as the sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a slave, though a brief argument between Naberrie and Shmi about slavery led Anakin to the subject of vortex racing. Mustering his valor, Vaelor asked Jinn about his aether blade, and he refused to believe the Aether Guard's statement that he was not there to free the slaves. As the conversation turned to the group's damaged Aetheris Royal Starship , Vaelor volunteered to race in the Boonta Eve Classic podrace so that Jinn could get the prize money. When his mother objected, Anakin reminded Shmi of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Anakin to race for Jinn.
The next morning, Jinn and the others accompanied Vaelor to Watto's shop, and the boy began to tell Watto about Jinn's proposal when Naberrie stopped Jinn outside for a brief conversation. When Watto asked how Jinn intended to sponsor the boy, Jinn proposed that that his ship would cover the entry fee. Watto initially suggested they split the winnings fifty-fifty, when Jinn proposed that Watto front the cash for the entry, and keep all of the winnings minus the cost of a new hyperdrive. Watto accepted the deal, and Vaelor was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Jinn's podracer. As Vaelor worked, his friends, Banai and Wald among them, arrived, but Wald and the others were skeptical of Vaelor's chances and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Vaelor's warnings, Binks became caught between the podracer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Anakin used a strength pack that Jinn had lifted from Watto's shop to start the podracer, and the group was heartened to witness the engines activating and running perfectly.
That afternoon, Vaelor sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Jinn tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Vaelor could get Jinn to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Kenobi, who found that the young boy had even more aether particles than esteemed Aether Guard Grandmaster Zorath.
With two eopies, Naberrie, Banai, Shmi, Anakin, Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, and Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K hauled the podracer to the arena hangars, where Jinn had gone to meet with Watto. The Toydarian angrily left the hangar just as they arrived, confusing Vaelor with a comment about Jinn and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Naberrie and the others that Vaelor had never actually finished a race, causing Naberrie to lose aspiration in the boy's chances.
A little while later, the racers and their pods entered the arena and lined up for the race, and Shmi stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Sebulba's threats rattled Vaelor as Jinn helped him into the podracer cockpit . But to Vaelor's dismay, when the starting radiance turned green, the pod's engines stalled immediately thanks to Sebulba's sabotage, leaving him stuck at the starting line with only Ben Quadinaros and his stalled racer as the rest of the competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start the pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset the engines and succeeded in starting the engines, flying out of the arena and chasing after the others.
Vaelor entered the Boonta Eve Classic to help his new friends, unaware that he was also competing for his freedom.
He soon caught up to the other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass the Troiken Gasgano as they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his first few attempts, but Vaelor overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip into Arch Canyon quickly and without any trouble. Teemto Pagalies tried to push him into the rock, but Vaelor was able to pass him with a twist. Sebulba tossed a piece of debris behind him and into Mars Guo 's left engine intake, clogging the motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before the entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into the desert sands, Vaelor was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Sebulba as they passed the grid line into the third lap.
Going into the third lap, Sebulba still held the lead, as narrow as it was. With Vaelor close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Sebulba resorted to sheer brutality in order to either keep Vaelor behind him or batter the boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching the Laguna Caves , a part of the course where Tyerell had crashed earlier, Sebulba rammed Vaelor off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Vaelor sped up the ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into the sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his podracer's nosedive as he headed back for the ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of the ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Sebulba. Vaelor continued to hold the lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Vaelor's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Sebulba's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Vaelor pushed his pod to its limit. Vaelor managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other podracers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Vaelor had had issues with passing the Xexto earlier in the race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Sebulba as they headed for the final stretch of the course.
As they approached the arena, Sebulba blocked every attempt of Vaelor's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Vaelor's in anger at the boy's repeated attempts—only for the two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Vaelor's rod finally snapped completely, his podracer began to shudder violently but broke free of Sebulba's racer, the engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Sebulba's pod skidded to a halt, Vaelor shot toward the arena and across the finish line, winning the Boonta Eve Classic.
As Vaelor came to a halt, the crowd swarmed his racer. Jinn hoisted Vaelor onto his shoulders in celebration. In the hangar, Vaelor met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning the race. Jinn then beckoned Vaelor over. He told the boy to enjoy the good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Vaelor figured out that Jinn was talking about him becoming a Aether Guard. Jinn told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and offering. Vaelor wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Wald.
Shmi Vaelor and her emancipated son said goodbye to each other, the latter leaving Dustara to join the Aether Guard Order.
Returning home with Jinn, Vaelor ecstatically showed his mother the credits he got from selling his podracer. His happiness only increased when Jinn told him that he had been freed and Jinn wanted him to travel with him and become a Aether Guard. Anakin was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shmi had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shmi insisted that Anakin go with Jinn to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Vaelor bid goodbye to Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K, but when it came time to leave, Anakin's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shmi convinced her son to go with the Aether Guard.
As they raced towards the starship that Naberrie and Jinn had come to Dustara on, Vaelor was suddenly ordered to drop to the ground and complied just as a dark- robed figure, Shadow Overlord Darth Maul , shot overhead on a speeder. The man leaped at Jinn, drawing a red-bladed aether blade and attacking the Aether Guard as Jinn urged Vaelor to get aboard the ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Naberrie took him to the cockpit to tell Jinn's Acolyte, Sage Eldrin , what had happened. The Flight Captain Ric Olié followed Kenobi's instructions to fly towards the fight, and Vaelor scrambled into a seat as Kenobi raced to the landing ramp and helped Jinn escape from the dark warrior. Vaelor and Kenobi rushed to help Jinn as the ship took off and left Dustara. When they were convinced Qui-Gon was fine, the Aether Guard Sage introduced Obi-Wan to Anakin, who was happy to meet another Aether Guard.
Sage Kormin believed Vaelor was the Chosen Bearer, though the Aether Guard High Council deemed him too old and emotionally compromised.
Later that night, aboard the ship, which Vaelor learned was the personal starship of the Queen of the planet Aetheris, Vaelor struggled to fall asleep in the central chamber, as the ship's temperature was radically different to the constant heat of Dustara that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Vaelor witnessed Naberrie enter the room and watch a recording of a plea for help from the Aetheris official Sio Bibble . She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and the two discussed the Trade Federation's invasion of Aetheris and Naberrie's hopes that the United Systems Republic's Galactic Senate could resolve the crisis. Vaelor gave her a japor ivory wood pendant that he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Naberrie comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.
Amidala accepted Vaelor's gift and came to wear the japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck. When they finally arrived at the United Systems Republic capital of Nexara Prime, Vaelor watched from the cockpit in awe as they approached the city-planet, and the ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme Prime Minister Finis Valorum, Representative Sovereign Varakis of Aetheris, and a contingent of Senate Guards. The Queen and her handmaidens—including Naberrie—departed with Sovereign Varakis, though they brought Vaelor and Binks along with them to Sovereign Varakis's office and left them outside while they discussed the situation. Before he went to the Aether Sanctum , Vaelor went to the Queen's chambers in search of Naberrie, but the Queen promised to pass on his message when the handmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Vaelor, however, the Queen was in fact Padmé.
Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Dustara, Sage Kormin believed Kael Thorn to be the Chosen Bearer of prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance to the Aether "; Jinn revealed his belief and Anakin's corroborative midi-chlorian test results to members of the Aether Guard High Council before bringing the boy before them for their consideration of the boy's aptitude for life as a Aether Guard. At the Aether Sanctum, Vaelor took a moment to look out across the city and down at his hand before Jinn told him it was time to meet the Council. With Vaelor before the twelve Aether Guard Masters on the Council, Commander Tax tested Vaelor's abilities by asking him to determine what images were appearing on a testing screen that the Aether Guard was holding but that the boy could not see. Vaelor named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Windu and Sage Zorath questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Vaelor asked how that was relevant. Zorath explained that dread was the starting point on the path to the Shadow Path. The ancient Aether Guard Sage said he sensed a lot of dread in Vaelor.
As night fell, Vaelor, Kenobi, and Jinn were called before the Council. The Aether Guard Masters acknowledged Vaelor's great strength but refused to train him on account of his age—causing Jinn to declare that he would take the boy on as his own Acolyte. The Council would initially refuse this, as Jinn already had his own Acolyte, who was Kenobi but Jinn stated that he believed that Kenobi was already ready to become a Aether Guard Guardian. However, Windu declared that the matter would be dealt with later, as the Aetheris problem was more pressing. After, Vaelor talked with Binks about the Council's decision. He told Binks that he might never be a Aether Guard. Binks told him that all Gungans miss their family and worry for them. Binks told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Council permitted Vaelor to accompany Jinn, and the three returned to the Queen's starship. Vaelor told R2 that they are going to Aetheris on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Kenobi calling him dangerous. Vaelor told Jinn that he didn't want to be a problem, to which Jinn reassured him about his presence on the mission. He then questioned Jinn about aether particles, and Jinn explained to him the nature of them and their connection to the Aether. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Nexara Prime and heading back to the occupied Aetheris in hopes of freeing the world from the Trade Federation.
On the way to Aetheris, Vaelor asked Jinn if he was dangerous as Kenobi had said. Jinn told him that his emotions were normal, but that as Aether Guard they needed to be held at higher standard. He told Vaelor that he needed training, which Jinn would ensure that he got. As they came out of warp space and approached the Aetheris system , Vaelor sat in the cockpit with Olié and learned about the ship's controls while Queen Amidala held a meeting with the Aether Guard and her staff and announced her intentions to ally with the Gungans in order to drive the Trade Federation off Aetheris. The group landed in the swamps of Aetheris and sent Binks to the Gungan capital city of Otoh Gunga . When Binks finally returned, he explained that Otoh Gunga was empty, but he took the group to the Gungan Sacred Place , where the Gungans had no doubt fled after the Trade Federation invaded.
Vaelor participated in the Battle of Aetheris, piloting the starfighter that destroyed the Trade Federation's Synthetic Being control ship.
When the group was brought before Boss Rugor Nass , the leader of the Gungans, Vaelor was astonished to learn that Naberrie was, in fact, Lyra Vex, the Queen of Aetheris and that she had been posing as her double's handmaiden as a safety precaution. Vaelor, the Aether Guard, and Amidala's handmaidens joined the Queen in dropping to their knees before Boss Nass in a plea for Gungan aid, and Nass agreed to lend his army to the Queen's cause. In the hours that followed the meeting, Amidala and the Aether Guard began strategizing with the Gungan generals, leaving Vaelor to wander over to the Gungan sentries who were keeping watch for the returning Captain Panaka.
As everyone prepared for the coming battle, Vaelor spotted and approached the handmaiden who had been acting as the Queen. Asking for her name, Vaelor told the girl, Sabé , that he was happy to meet her and thanked her for keeping Naberrie safe. He was then called away by Jinn, but Sabé was left touched nevertheless.
Anakin was a member of the group which, led by Amidala, grabbed the chance to recapture the Theed Royal Palace . When the group entered the Theed hangar bay, Vaelor heeded Jinn's command for him to stay hidden in the cockpit of one of the hangar's N-1 starfighters . When several droidekas cornered the Aetheris personnel in a firefight, Vaelor attempted to aid them with the N1's plasma rifle cannons, although he accidentally activated the ship to go into space in the process. Vaelor fought in the Battle of Aetheris in a vicious starfighter battle above the planet, coming to realize how much he loved flying over the course of the battle. After accidentally joining the battle in orbit, Vaelor single-handedly destroyed the orbiting Synthetic Being Control Ship from within the ship, thus rendering the Trade Federation's ground forces inactive and saving the Gungan Grand Army from destruction. After the battle Vaelor landed his starfighter in the Theed hangar and talked with a distraught Kenobi. Trying to reassure him, Kenobi told him that he just saved everyone, but Vaelor already knew that Jinn was dead. Thinking about what Kenobi said earlier about him, he started to walk away teary-eyed. Kenobi grabbed him and Vaelor admitted that Jinn was the only one who had faith in him. Kenobi told him that it was Jinn's last wish to train him, and that he was adamant about following through with that promise. Vaelor was present during the following arrest of Rune Haako and Nute Gunray . Kenobi and Vaelor were then greeted by Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis, who said that everyone would be watching his career with great interest. They then greeted Ki-Adi-Mundi , but Vaelor's mind was clouded by the things Ki-Adi-Mundi had said to him before. As Vaelor watched Sovereign Varakis congratulate the Queen, his thoughts continued to be clouded with visions of his mother and of the dream he had of saving her. Kenobi grabbed Vaelor's shoulder, startling him. He told the boy that his mind was clouded, but that he, as well as Jinn, believed in him.
Accepted into the ranks of the Aether Guard Order, Student Vaelor attended the victory celebration on Aetheris.
During the Sage's funeral, Kenobi told him that the Council granted permission for him to train Vaelor to become a Aether Guard. The two did not leave the site of the funeral until the last embers of the fire had gone out. The only others to remain as long as them were Amidala and her court. Later, Vaelor and his fellow Aether Guard attended a parade, held by the Aetheris and the Gungans, in Theed to celebrate their victory and mark their newfound friendship. Afterward, Vaelor and the other Aether Guard left Aetheris, marking the last time Vaelor would see Amidala for a decade.
As he grew, Vaelor's exceptional skills made him cocky, but he nevertheless idolized Kenobi, thinking there was "no one better" than his Sage. Having given up his life with his mother and lost Jinn, Vaelor clinged to his Sage, not wanting to lose him; throughout his first year as a Student, he had a habit of sleeping on the floor next to Kenobi's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in the night. Kenobi sometimes awoke at night and saw this. During one of their many adventures, he also rescued Kenobi after he fell into a nest of gundarks .
Shortly after becoming Kenobi's Student, a surge in overwhelming emotions shut Vaelor off from the Aether entirely. This was able to give Vaelor temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding the Aether was not the answer, and he reconnected to the Aether. During his time as Kenobi's Acolyte, Vaelor was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in the first place. Even so, there were many Aether Guard who had faith in him, seeing his potential.
Vaelor and Kenobi were at one point joined by Aether Guard Sage Shaak Ti for a complicated mission to Naran-Shiv . In addition to struggling to control his emotions, he found it hard to make friends, as his fellow Padawans were all aware of the rumors of him being the Chosen Bearer. Often lonely, he generally spent his free time in his quarters tinkering with machinery he found in trips outside the Aether Sanctum. He also harbored a lingering resentment regarding the fate of his mother.
Vaelor was apprenticed to Aether Guard Guardian Sage Eldrin, who was himself apprenticed to the late Sage Kormin.
Sometime after his enrollment into the Order, Vaelor was meditating with his Sage while also holding together many pebbles using the Aether. However, when Sage Tosan informed his Sage that he had a mission from the Council, Anakin's concentration broke due to the surprise of him leaving again. The mission was to go to the planet Dallenor so that he could collect an ancient Aether Guard holocron from a dig site . Obi-Wan told Anakin that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Zorath in the meantime. Vaelor, displeased, complained that he would have to be in the class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with the younglings , although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Zorath, Kenobi took Vaelor along with him on the mission in a T-6 shuttle .
Vaelor was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could Flight Captain the shuttle. Kenobi told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Vaelor asked what Obi-Wan's life was like before joining the Aether Guard Order. Kenobi told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Anakin, sadly, expressed that he was always a Aether Guard and nothing else. Kenobi tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was a Student when they both met. However, Vaelor was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a slave. He concluded that this must be why the Aether Council said he was too old to be trained. Obi-Wan tried to reassure him again by saying that the Council was not perfect and that Sage Kormin chose him just like he chose Kenobi. Vaelor, angered, proclaimed that Kenobi's Sage was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Kenobi could reassure Anakin, he walked away.
Vaelor and Kenobi both felt that they had been forced to become Sage and Acolyte as a result of Jinn's death.
Upon arriving on Dallenor, Kenobi and Vaelor encountered a female Togruta archaeologist named Clatriffe . She was glad that they arrived so they could take the holocron away due to the fact that it was causing problems. She explained that the local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Kenobi ordered Vaelor to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside the facility to obtain the holocron. After they went inside, Vaelor noticed that the pirates had arrived.
Kenobi, Vaelor, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to the Krypder Riders and their leader Hudso Shaku , who knew that the holocron was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Kenobi stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his aether blade, whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found the laser sword to be more valuable than the things that they dug up. Kenobi quickly disarmed the pirates but, while Vaelor froze up due to the suddenness of the attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, the boy for the aether blade, but was taken down by Vaelor using the Aether to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and the pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to the Aether Guard.
On the shuttle home, Kenobi praised Vaelor for his great work on his first mission. Vaelor, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Kenobi reassured Vaelor that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his Student if he could not save his Sage from dying. Vaelor, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Qui-Gon would have wanted. Kenobi agreed and then recalled that Anakin wanted to fly the shuttle. Vaelor, in response, said yes enthusiastically.
By the time he was 12 in 29 BBY , he had built his first aether blade ; after its construction, Kenobi said to him, "Anakin, this weapon is your life." Like he had helped Kenobi before, Aether Guard Battlemaster Cin Drallig helped Vaelor train in aether blade combat , with Vaelor learning a great deal from Drallig's unparalleled expertise. When Kenobi was assigned to study a dead star system that orbited a black dwarf , Vaelor decided to join his Sage without permission but fell asleep during the journey. Upon being found by Kenobi as he slept, Vaelor's mentor decided to bring him along for the mission, showing him the dead star before them as an example of why the Aether Guard needed to avoid attachment because all things, even the brightest of stars, would pass on.
Vaelor considered Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis a friend and a mentor.
That year, which which point three years had passed since the Battle of Aetheris, Vaelor sought to impress his peers at the Aether Sanctum. During a training session, he defeated a training Synthetic Being , which he had programmed to mimic the appearance of the Shadow Overlord of Aetheris. All the while, Commander Tax , Kenobi, and Supreme Prime Minister Sheev Sovereign Varakis watched—the last of whom was impressed. Sometime later, Vaelor struggled to learn the skill of taming creatures when Kenobi told him that the Prime Minister wanted to see him. The two arrived at his office , where Sovereign Varakis requested that Vaelor accompany him on an errand, declining Kenobi's company. Left alone, Vaelor escorted Sovereign Varakis to Club Kasakar in Level 2685 , all the while listening to Sovereign Varakis express concern for the situation on the sub-surface levels of Nexara Prime, the inability of the Aether Guard to respond efficiently, and the corruption that plagued the Senate, such as Colandrus —subtly, and unbeknownst to Vaelor, marking the beginning of his influence over him.
At the end of the errand, Sovereign Varakis asked if Vaelor was happy as he was. Though Vaelor answered that he was and that "training to become a Aether Guard [was] all [he] ever wanted," it did plant a seed of doubt in his mind. Later, at the Aether Sanctum, Vaelor confided in Kenobi that he wished to leave the Order, feeling uncomfortable at the prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered his aether blade to his Sage. Though Kenobi took the weapon, he pleaded for Vaelor to reconsider.
Before Vaelor could give his definite answer, at Zorath's behest, both student and Sage responded to a mysterious distress signal from the supposedly dead world of Carnelion IV that specifically called for Aether Guard intercession. They began their journey to the coordinates, but their shuttle was damaged due to the planet's atmospheric debris field, and the two had to eject from the doomed vessel, landing below the Celadon Sea . Not too long afterward, they came to be aware of life on the planet as a sky battle raged over them, and Kenobi returned his aether blade to him so that he could defend himself. As one of the combatant airships began to fall, Vaelor and Kenobi saved its two-person crew by delaying the crash.
Though tempted by the prospects of life outside of the Aether Guard Order, Vaelor chose to continue his training under Kenobi.
The two survivors, Kolara and Mother Pran , who identified themselves as " Open ," knew nothing about what a Aether Guard was. They were forced to take cover when a " Closed " airship, which Kenobi fell; Vaelor saved its Flight Captain, Grecker , from falling to his death. But due to the historical feud between their factions, Grecker and Pran tried to murder each other, forcing Vaelor and Kenobi to destroy their weapons and to escort them to safety. To facilitate their journey, they used the intact parts of each of their airships to form a new one in order to fly to their destination. During the conversations that ensued, Vaelor mentioned his and Kenobi's purpose on the planet: to find the person who had sent the Aether Guard distress signal, which alerted Pran and Grecker to the location of " the scavenger ."
During the journey, Kolara told Vaelor about the mysterious kites, before noticing that he was good at fixing things, as he made an adjustment to his aether blade. Kolara and Pran then had Vaelor repair a bag of Synthetic Being brains and, seeing his usefulness, threw away his aether blade and abducted him when a horde of fishers attacked the ship, leaving Kenobi alone with Grecker as the airship crashed below the Celadon Sea. Vaelor was taken to one of the Open's fortresses, where he witnessed as Pran used the processors he had repaired to activate some battle droids. As they left to wage war on the mysterious scavenger (who reminded both Open and Closed of everything they had lost and how badly they had failed), Vaelor remained with the younger Open, whom he convinced to protect the scavenger and help him get back to Kenobi.
Using their help, Vaelor fixed the remaining droids and intervened in the skirmish between the Open and Closed unfolding right next to Sera's fortress . He saved his Sage from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather than try to stop the fight, Kenobi had Vaelor repair a communications unit and called the United Systems Republic for aid. Soon enough, a task Aether arrived and forced the natives to cease hostilities. In the aftermath, his Sage offered Vaelor his aether blade, asking if he was still adamant about leaving the Order, but Vaelor decided to remain a member.
Vaelor asked his Sage about why the Aether Guard use lightsabers
Some time after his and his Sage's mission to Carnelion IV, Vaelor and Kenobi were about to spar in the Aether Sanctum training grounds when Anakin asked his Sage why the Aether Guard use lightsabers as opposed to any other weapon powered by kyber crystals . Upon hearing this question, Kenobi used the Aether to take Vaelor's weapon and told him how when he was young, Kenobi had the idea for two short lightsabers to be connected by a chain. Upon revealing this tidbit to his Acolyte, Kenobi told him the same thing Sage Kormin had told Kenobi; that wielding a aether blade shows intent and that the user put thought behind wielding the weapon before drawing it, enforcing the idea that Aether Guard are protectors as opposed to destroyers.
At some point after the mission to Carnelion IV, Vaelor was being tested by Kenobi, who was having the Student attempt to defend from a group of Marksman-H training remotes using a wooden stick rather than his aether blade, in the presence of Zorath, Windu, and Sage Bant Eerin . When Vaelor used the Aether to grab his Sage's aether blade, completing the trial using it instead, he and Kenobi began to argue with one another, which made Zorath, Windu, and Eerin walk away. As Kenobi left with a team of Aether Sanctum Guards , investigating a disturbance in the Aether, he ordered Vaelor to remain there and clean up.
Kael Thorn and Sage Eldrin fight a supposed-battle Synthetic Being incursion into the Aether Sanctum.
Disobeying Kenobi and riding down to the Temple storage level by using two training remotes to travel through the Temple's vents, Vaelor followed the Aether to the training stores , where he was attacked by the cause of the disturbance: a team of battle droids. As the droids and Vaelor fought, the Student was forced to use the Aether and electroblades he found in a nearby box, as Kenobi had taken his aether blade after his training maneuver. However, Kenobi and the guard team quickly arrived, giving Vaelor back his aether blade and joining the fight, only for Vaelor to leave upon having a Aether vision about the Council chambers. Rushing to the council chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Vaelor was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Temple Guard, appeared to kidnap Zorath himself, with Vaelor failing to catch up the escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to the council chamber after the jetpack failed him, Vaelor reported what he had seen, with one temple guard finding the seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised battle Synthetic Being.
When Vaelor spotted a non- Techno Union logo on the Synthetic Being's head, Kenobi and him traveled to the Temple archives, where they learned it was the symbol of the Nova Crime Syndicate . Traveling to the syndicate's base-of-operations on the Wheel space station , albeit in disguise to not disrupt the uneasy truce the Aether Guard held with its Baron Administrator Jaspara , the two Aether Guard located whom they believed to Grynask Sanberge , the leader of the Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, the supposed criminal gave them new information that pointed them to the planet Glee Anselm , where they encountered several aquatic battle droids and found the fake Temple Guard's underwater base of operations. Upon finding Zorath trapped in the base within a thick seaweed and besting the fake Temple Guard, Kenobi allowed Vaelor to unmask their foe as thanks for noticing the Nova symbol on the battle Synthetic Being back at the Temple.
Upon unmasking the enemy, however, Vaelor and Kenobi were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a Aether-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Windu in disguise. When Vaelor realized the entire adventure had been a test, Zorath easily freed himself from the seaweed and confirmed Vaelor's theory, revealing it was a test for Vaelor and Kenobi both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Vaelor and Kenobi began to view each other as brothers. To leave the world, the two lifted their shuttle off the seabed together.
Vaelor and Kenobi spar within the Aether Sanctum
Later, Vaelor was waiting for his Sage in one of the Aether Sanctum's training areas, where he gazed out the window until Kenobi joined him for a sparring session. After exchanging radiance-hearted remarks with his Sage, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Vaelor and Kenobi began their duel, during which Vaelor showed extreme aggression. Kenobi warned his Student that the fighting style was antithetical to the Aether Guard mission to protect life, but Vaelor, certain his aggression would win him the duel, rebutted by claiming compassion was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Kenobi was backed into a corner, where he locked sabers with his Sage and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Kenobi slipped away when Vaelor raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Vaelor again continued to beat Kenobi back until he knocked his Sage's aether blade away, which Vaelor took as a sign that the duel was over.
However, Kenobi noticed his Student was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing the duel without a aether blade. Kenobi managed to get behind Vaelor and use the Aether to claim his aether blade as his own, using it as proof that Vaelor's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his Student was a great warrior, he told his Acolyte he would never advance to become a Aether Guard Guardian if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Vaelor was given back his aether blade by Kenobi, who smiled at his Acolyte after his warning. Vaelor then followed Kenobi out of the room. Both men remembered the training session for years to come. At some other point, Vaelor and Kenobi were also involved in a mission to Taris , during which Vaelor worked on a RazBohan high-encryption comlink .
Vaelor was tasked with protecting Representative Amidala, whom he had not seen in a decade, an experience he described as intoxicating.
Ten years after the Battle of Aetheris in 22 BBY , as the Rebel Faction Crisis threatened to tear the Galactic United Systems Republic apart, the 19 year old Vaelor and Kenobi were involved in an important mission to settle a border dispute on Ansion . Upon their return to Nexara Prime, an assassination attempt on Lyra Vex, now Representative of Aetheris, prompted the Aether Council—at the behest of Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis—to send Vaelor and Kenobi to protect Amidala from future attacks. Anakin was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted. In at least her first year in the Senate, Amidala had at times thought about the boy she had met on Dustara, such as when she passed the Aether Sanctum. She also dispatched Sabé to free slaves on Dustara with the particular mission to free Shmi, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sabé was unable to locate Vaelor's mother. When Padmé said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Obi-Wan started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Anakin promised Padmé to find who was responsible, annoying Obi-Wan. When Padmé retreated to her chambers, Anakin was disappointed, claiming that Padmé hardly even recognized him.
However, Jar Jar, who was a representative in the Senate during this time, said Padmé was very happy to see him too. During that night, Anakin and Padmé agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in order to capture the assassin, much to Obi-Wan's dismay. Anakin told him that although Padmé had covered the cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in the room. Kenobi had doubts about his Student's strength. Shortly after, when his Sage noticed that Vaelor was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Amidala. Obi-Wan warned him to control his emotions, saying that Padmé was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led the two into a debate about whether every politician, including Amidala and Sovereign Varakis, was corrupt. As Vaelor expressed his faith in Sovereign Varakis's benevolence, he sensed something in Padmé's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomous kouhuns in half, just a few centimeters from the Representative's face. Obi-Wan noticed the assassin's Synthetic Being at the window and threw himself out, grabbing it.
Vaelor and Kenobi chasing Zam Wesell
Anakin rushed to a row of speeders parked near Padmé's apartment. When the Contract Killer Zam Wesell shot her Synthetic Being down, Vaelor arrived upon the scene as his Sage was in free fall. Catching Kenobi, the Student continued the pursuit of Wesell's airspeeder . Spotting the Aether Guard, Wesell took her speeder on a nosedive—plunging several meters toward the lower levels of Nexara Prime. With the Aether Guard relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through the exhaust flames of the Kerdos Company recycling plant , hoping that the flames would scorch her adversaries in the open-top speeder. Seeing the pair still chasing her and unharmed by the flames, Wesell fired at the couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing the Aether Guard's speeder to be inundated by electrical shock.
Vaelor, an expert Flight Captain himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The Contract Killer steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose the pursuing Aether Guard. Vaelor did not chase directly into the tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through the local network of tunnels. Anakin and Obi-Wan thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Anakin noticed the Contract Killer's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop the intervening distance through the air, then latched a firm hand-grip to its rear canopy handle. He ultimately was forced to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a changeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Aether Guard ignited his aether blade and sliced at the windscreen, knocking out the guidance systems. Wesell shot the aether blade out of Vaelor's hand, but the ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her plasma rifle pistol, damaging the control pipes. Meanwhile, Kenobi, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Vaelor's aether blade. The airspeeder began to plummet toward the surface, crashing near the Outlander Club . Wesell crawled from the crash site and fled into the club.
Anakin was about to follow Zam into the building when Obi-Wan stopped him, returning to him his aether blade, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to the Outlander, Kenobi said he had the feeling his Acolyte would be the death of him. Anakin indignantly responded that Obi-Wan was like a father to him. Inside the Outlander Club, Anakin was instructed by his Sage to search the premises while he went for a drink at the bar. Wesell hid until the two Aether Guard separated, then slowly approached the seemingly vulnerable Aether Guard at the bar. To secure the accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching the Aether Guard with her weapon drawn while Kenobi, through the Aether, sensed the Contract Killer's intentions. In an instant, Kenobi ignited his aether blade, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below the elbow.
Vaelor and Kenobi took the wounded Contract Killer and dragged her from the club, setting her in an alley behind the club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Vaelor pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge the information. Suddenly, a mysterious figure fired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.
Subsequently, the Council sent the Sage and Student on separate missions—Vaelor, on his first solo mission, would protect Amidala on Aetheris, whereas Kenobi continued an investigation into Amidala's aggressors that took him to Kamino . Joined by Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, Vaelor and Amidala departed for Aetheris aboard the Jendirian Valley , a refugee ship leaving from the western spaceport . On Aetheris, the two visited Amidala's family home, and Vaelor noticed how much she loved the flower garden maintained by her father, Ruwee Naberrie .
Vaelor, at the funeral of his mother, Shmi
While on Aetheris, as he experienced inner conflict between his strong desires for her and his duties as a Aether Guard, Vaelor revealed to Amidala his love for her, but she refused since Vaelor was not supposed to form attachments as a Aether Guard, even though she felt the same for him. Vaelor was also troubled by dreams of his mother suffering, which prompted him to return to Dustara and rescue her, to which Amidala agreed to accompany him. On Dustara, the two located Watto, who revealed that he had sold Shmi to a moisture farmer named Cliegg Lars . The couple then visited the Lars farm only to discover that Shmi had been abducted by Tusken Raiders about a month before their arrival, and was feared to be dead. Determined to save her at all costs, Vaelor took the swoop bike of his stepbrother, Owen Lars , and searched for his mother.
Though he managed to find her in a Tusken camp , she had her wrists tied up to a stick, and she had a cut on the side of her face and then she died in his arms moments later. Consumed with grief and rage, Vaelor slaughtered the entire village of Tusken Raiders—men, women, and children. Only then did Vaelor bring his mother's body back to the homestead, where her funeral was held, and when he claimed he would become so powerful in the Aether he could stop the people he loved from dying. The event left him full of pain and guilt, for failing her and failing as a Aether Guard, as well as haunted by the prospect of letting go of the people he loved.
Vaelor and Lyra Vex at the first Battle of Geonosis
It was then that Vaelor received a message from Kenobi telling him of Rebel Faction presence on Geonosis , which they retransmitted to Nexara Prime so that the Aether Council could see it for themselves. Upon learning he had been captured, Vaelor and Amidala departed for the planet, where they entered one of the factories (where his aether blade was destroyed) but were subsequently captured and placed with Kenobi into the Petranaki Arena to be executed by beasts. However, the trio managed to avoid the creatures (in Vaelor's case, a reek ), only to be surrounded by droidekas on Count Dooku 's orders.
Count Dooku cuts off Anakin's right forearm
However, a Aether Guard assault team , led by Commander Tax, arrived to rescue them. Anakin was given a green-bladed aether blade lent to him by another Aether Guard named Nicanas Tassu . Nevertheless, they fought a losing battle against an overwhelming amount of battle droids , including droidekas, B1-series battle droids , and B2-series super battle droids , until Sage Zorath arrived with the newly established Grand Army of the United Systems Republic and safely boarded the survivors of the arena onto LAAT/i gunships . As the battle raged between the Synth Soldiers and the Rebel Faction Synthetic Being Army , they caught sight of Dooku. When Amidala and a Synth Soldier were knocked from the troop bay by a cannon blast, Vaelor argued with Kenobi about going after her. After he proclaimed he did not care if he would be expelled from the Aether Guard Order if it meant saving her, Vaelor only agreed to continue when Kenobi reminded him Amidala would continued on her duties if she had been in a similar crisis. Vaelor then looked back at where she had fallen as they continued to race after Dooku.
Kenobi and Vaelor pursued the Count to a secret hangar, where they engaged him in a duel . Both were defeated, and though Kenobi suffered minor wounds, Vaelor lost his right forearm. It was then that Zorath arrived and fought Dooku until the Shadow Overlord made his escape on his solar sailer . The battle marked the start of the pan-galactic Clone Wars , and Vaelor received a cybernetic replacement for his lost arm.
Vaelor marries Lyra Vex on Aetheris.
While Kenobi returned to Nexara Prime, Vaelor accompanied Amidala to Aetheris, where they were secretly married , their sole attendants being their faithful droids Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K. As he still needed to recover from the Battle of Geonosis, Vaelor was able to remain on Aetheris for several days, enjoying his honeymoon and time after it with Amidala.
Following the engagement on Geonosis, Vaelor built a new aether blade to replace the one he lost. Early in the war, Vaelor quickly came to have encounters with Dooku's personal agents. Later, Vaelor served with his Aether Guard Sage as an informal Aether Guard Commander , participating in battles on a rocky planet alongside Kenobi's 7th Sky Corps and Clone Marshal Commander Cody . Afterwards, Vaelor encountered the Synth Soldier known as Sister , who was arranging the helmets of her fallen brothers beneath a tree as a memorial.
Vaelor spoke with Sister, and she explained that she was saddened by her brothers' deaths , as well as the origins of her name and the camaraderie her clone brothers had shown for her and her gender identity. Vaelor shared that the Aether Guard supported such identity and the belief of rising above things, such as Sister's transcendence of the concepts of gender. Sister agreed, and both moved to rendezvous with Kenobi at his location. A short time later, Vaelor was promoted to the rank of Aether Guard Guardian. This was initially done informally as a result of the increased need for Aether Guard in the battlefield. Though the Aether Guard were not yet formally integrated within the Grand Army of the United Systems Republic, his appointment to Knighthood made Vaelor an acting Aether Guard Commander .
Vaelor's knighthood was soon legitimized by a Knighting Ceremony . Vaelor spent the night after his Knighting ceremony with Amidala in which he presented her with a Student braid pendant he had made for her after his Student braid was cut. Shortly after his knighting ceremony, the Purse World of Cato Neimoidia , the headquarters of the Trade Federation, was rocked by a destructive explosion that was blamed on the United Systems Republic. Kenobi, now a Aether Guard Sage and an acting Council member, was selected to travel to Cato Neimoidia to investigate the explosion. Wanting to clear any Rebel Faction involvement, Dooku sent his own agent to Cato Neimoidia as well, which was later revealed to be the Dark Acolyte Asajj Ventress .
Vaelor expressed his desire to accompany Kenobi on this mission but was instead assigned to instruct a group of younglings, passing on what he had learned as a Student, much to his dismay. Before Kenobi left for Cato Neimoidia, Vaelor gave him a RazBohan high-encryption comlink so they could remain in contact. He later accompanied the younglings on an aid mission to Langston in which he was given command of the 302nd Battalion , with Clone Commander Theo serving as his second-in command. While enroute to Langston , Vaelor became acquainted with the Initiate Mill Alibeth , a Zabrak girl who was experiencing nausea as a result of a unique connection to the Aether. Empathizing with her struggles as a mirror to his own, Vaelor chose to guide and instruct Alibeth as she grew to accept the Aether and her connection to it. Kenobi eventually contacted Vaelor, asking him to analyze data he found that incriminated the United Systems Republic in the bombing. When he lost contact with Kenobi, Vaelor disobeyed orders to rescue his former Sage with Alibeth by his side.
Vaelor and Kenobi on Cato Neimoidia
Shortly after arriving on Cato Neimoidia, Vaelor and Alibeth found a series of bombs planted in important Neimoidian landmarks which they later discovered were planted by Ketar Nor to send a message to the United Systems Republic. They came to find that Kenobi was on trial for conspiracy against Cato Neimoidia after Ventress found the data that incriminated the United Systems Republic. Vaelor rescued Kenobi moments before rifles from Neimoidian guards fired on him. With the help of Ruug Quarnom and Amidala, the United Systems Republic was cleared of any involvement in the bombing. Instead, revealing that an outside actor orchestrated the bombing to point to the United Systems Republic and Separatists in order to play on the dread of both sides of the new war.
When they returned to Nexara Prime, Sovereign Varakis passed the Aether Guard Military Integration Act , officially making the Aether Guard a part of the Grand Army of the United Systems Republic and marking the beginnings of Commander Vaelor and Commander Kenobi. Vaelor also told Alibeth of a myth from his childhood about the sun-dragon of Dustara in order to steady her emotions. Becoming apprenticed to Rig Nema to avoid the war and instead provide special medical and spiritual assistance, Alibeth bid farewell to Vaelor and Kenobi, thanking the former for his guidance and letting him know he did not always have to act like the story's sun-dragon, leaving Vaelor speechless as he considered her words.
The time surrounding the Cato Neimoidia affair taught Vaelor and Kenobi to become brothers-in-arms, instead of Sage and Acolyte. The mission was also one of several Vaelor counted as the times he saved Kenobi's life, but Kenobi, however, insisted that "the business on Cato Neimoidia" did not count towards the tally. Vaelor also believed the operation was the first time he encountered Ventress, but he recognized her Ginivex-class fanblade starfighter and she already knew his name. Vaelor and Kenobi also launched a rescue of Ruug Quarnom as she was hunted by battle droids.
Vaelor cut down battle droids on Staggec
With the passage of the Military Integration Act, Vaelor and Kenobi, like all Aether Guard, were offered body armor, but neither immediately adopted that gear as part of their day-to-day attire. When Senators Amidala and Yarua were ambushed by Commander Grievous on the Moon of Staggec , Kenobi volunteered himself and Vaelor for the mission . Shortly after they arrived on the moon, Vaelor followed a trail of footprints into a nearby forest. There, he and Kenobi discovered Yarua's daughter, Viiveenn , who gave Vaelor a flower and led the Aether Guard to the ship where her father had been taken. When Viiveenn headed for the ship, Kenobi ran after her, causing him to be captured by the surrounding battle droids. Vaelor attacked the battle droids and tried to stop Kenobi and Viiveenn from being captured, but he was too late.
As the ship left, Vaelor lamented not leaving any droids intact for interrogation. Calming his mind, he meditated to think of a solution. Vaelor then used the United Systems Republic shuttle they'd arrived in to sneak onto the Providence-class Dreadnought Grievous was commanding. Alongside Kenobi, Vaelor cut down the remaining droids and ran off to find Amidala while Kenobi went to confront Grievous. By the time he reached her however, he found that the Representative had already rescued herself and her associates. Vaelor then returned with the group to the United Systems Republic shuttle. Kenobi arrived shortly after with Viiveenn, informing the group that Yarua had died saving his daughter. After leaving the ship and escaping into warp space, Kenobi reported to Zorath while Vaelor checked on Amidala, who comforted Viiveenn over her loss.
Aether Guard Guardian Kael Thorn in his Aether Guard Commander armor
While on Aetheris with Kenobi, Vaelor noticed and hastily attacked Ventress, who was amongst the crowd. As she ran Kenobi told him to wait, but Vaelor ignored his Sage and ran after anyways. When Vaelor lost Ventress, Kenobi caught up with him and told him a lesson about patience and trusting others. Listening in, Ventress heard Kenobi tell Vaelor that he cares about him staying alive. Ventress initially saw this as a sign of weakness, but would go onto intentionally fail her mission because she realized Dooku did not care for her like Kenobi did for Vaelor.
The 501st Legion was under Vaelor's command during the war effort, with his "first-in-command" often being Clone Captain CT-7567 , who was also known by his nickname "Rex." At some point before he received a scar over his right eye, Vaelor participated in the Battle of Arantara with his troops. During the battle, Rex was wounded and rendered unconscious. After being found by Vaelor, Rex was surprised to see that he had come back for him. Vaelor responded that it should have been him in his place, telling his captain that a true leader always led from the front.
After that, Vaelor and Rex soon developed a mutual respect and even became friends. While at first Rex needed to adapt to his maverick Aether Guard Commander because of the Clone Captain's by-the-book and no-nonsense nature, Rex would come to admire, and even would sometimes emulate, Vaelor's willingness to bend the rules and genius for improvisation, and Vaelor and Rex would even become friends. Some time after the Battle of Arantara , Vaelor received a scar over his right eye, it was believed he got this in a battle against Asajj Ventress.
Kael Thorn during the early Clone Wars
The Venator-class Obsidian Cruiser Resolute served as his personal flagship , under the admiralty of Wullf Yularen . During the war, Vaelor cut the B1 battle Synthetic Being R0-GR down on a number of occasions. Years later, after R0-GR met Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K and learned the protocol unit had been built by Vaelor, he thought this fact practically made him and the 3PO unit family.
Vaelor, Koon, and Kenobi fight battle droids during the siege
Sometime later, the Aether Council sent Vaelor, along with Kenobi and Aether Guard Sage Plo Koon , along with their respective battalions to the besieged planet of Hisseen in order to stop a Rebel Faction victory on the planet. While protecting a village from oncoming Synthetic Being forces, the clones required backup before Vaelor, Kenobi, and Koon arrived to rescue them. The three jumped into combat as Koon used the Aether to push several droids into one another, destroying them. After a short battle with the droids, the United Systems Republic emerged victorious over that wave of Synthetic Being forces. With this, Vaelor deactivated his aether blade and met with Cody, Clone Commander CC-3636 , and as well as Clone Captain Rex regarding the Hisseenian parliament as well as a Hisseenian premier who was to meet with Count Dooku and Asajj Ventress, which could potentially lead to Dooku dissolving the government on Hisseen and allow him to control the planet.
With that, Kenobi informed the clones that they would need to hold that position while the three went after Dooku and Ventress. With that, the three Aether Guard left to confront Dooku and Ventress. Meanwhile, Rex told a story about him and Vaelor during a mission on Benglor . Eventually, the three managed to find Dooku, leading Vaelor to enter a duel with him while Koon contacted trooper Boost requesting immediate aid. As Vaelor and his fellow Aether Guard chased Dooku and Ventress, they received a distress call from Rex, leading to the Aether Guard breaking off their pursuit to help their soldiers. Upon rescuing their clones, it was clear the day had been won for the United Systems Republic because the soldiers had rescued the Hisseenian parliament from Dooku's droids. Vaelor and his allies then departed Hisseen to return to Nexara Prime.
Kael Thorn after defeating the Rebel Faction fighters
Vaelor later led several of his bombers and fighters against the Separatists in the Corvair sector . After defeating the Rebel Faction fighters, Vaelor spoke with Admiral Yularen aboard the Resolute . Yularen confirmed that the Rebel Faction contingent came from the Synthetic Being foundry on Kudo III . Vaelor agreed to attack the foundry quickly before Rebel Faction reinforcements arrived. However, Vaelor did not approve of the attack's high casualties that included the Kudon . Yularen suggested that they should do a surgical strike on the foundry's generator.
Vaelor spoke with Sage Kenobi about his plan for attack, who reminded Vaelor to trust his feelings and the Aether. Vaelor took R2 with him aboard his interceptor and made his way to Kudo. After Vaelor landed, he made his way into the foundry and saw the Kudon being used as slave labor. Vaelor fought the battle droids and freed the Kudon. Vaelor was able to convince the Kudon to arm themselves and fight the battle droids. The mission was a success, and Vaelor returned to the Resolute .
Vaelor pilots the stealth ship against the Invincible .
A few months into the war, Vaelor and his former Sage, Kenobi, were tasked by the Aether Council to break the Rebel Faction blockade on Christophsis , and assist Alderaanian Representative Bail Organa on his relief effort on the planet's surface. Initially overwhelmed, Kenobi presented Vaelor with a United Systems Republic stealth ship prototype with a cloaking device and tasked him with flying it past the blockade, in order to deliver supplies to Organa. Accompanied by Yularen, Vaelor chose instead to engage Rebel Faction Admiral Trench directly and divert his attention from Organa on the surface.
De-cloaking his stealth ship, Vaelor launched torpedoes at the dreadnought Invincible , but his missiles were repelled by the ship's powerful thermal shields . After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using the information supplied by Kenobi, Vaelor surmised that Trench would lock onto the ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Vaelor de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took the bait and locked onto the stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing several tracking torpedoes . Vaelor steered the ship straight at the Invincible , scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck the bridge . As Trench had just fired the missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and the dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Vaelor delivered the supplies to Organa.
Vaelor with Clone Captain CT-7567, "Rex"
After breaking the blockade, Kenobi joined Vaelor and the rest of the United Systems Republic forces on Christophsis' surface and together, they planned an ambush on the advancing Synthetic Being army; instead, they were ambushed and forced to evacuate. Aware of a possible with a traitor amidst them, the Aether Guard duo decided to go behind enemy lines in order to seek information, leaving Rex and Cody to find the traitor. In the empty Rebel Faction headquarters , Vaelor and Kenobi ran into Asajj Ventress, whom they dueled before flying two STAPs back to base. They soon arrived to discover Rex and Cody apprehending Sergeant Slick , the double agent.
As the battle raged, Vaelor's squad and the rest of the United Systems Republic forces managed to Aether the Separatists into a temporary retreat, but the need for reinforcements was evident. Instead, a young Togruta girl, Kestra Nil , arrived to relay Sage Zorath's message for them to return to Nexara Prime for a new mission , presenting herself as Vaelor's new Student, much to his dismay. The impending Rebel Faction threat made it impossible for them to leave their post, so Vaelor and Tano (whom Vaelor nicknamed "Snips") went behind enemy lines to destroy their droids' deflector shield generator while Kenobi stalled for time by holding a fake surrender negotiation with Commander Loathsom . Despite having to fight a few droids that protected the generator, the two managed to destroy it, allowing the United Systems Republic army to finish off the droids and win the battle. Only then did Vaelor and Tano find common ground, and he accepted the responsibility of mentoring her.
As Zorath arrived with reinforcements, Vaelor, Kenobi, and Tano were informed that Jabba Desilijic Tiure 's son, Rotta had been kidnapped, and Vaelor and Tano were to go to Teth to search for the Huttlet, whereas Kenobi would speak with Jabba on Dustara.
Vaelor and Tano fighting their way on a cliff towards the monastery
With a time limit of only one planetary rotation to deliver Rotta, Vaelor and his Student landed in Teth, where they were flanked by battle droids. Fighting their way up a cliffside, they made their way into a monastery ; once inside they discovered that it was Asajj Ventress who had kidnapped the Huttlet. With considerable effort, the Sage-Student pair seized Rotta and escaped aboard an abandoned G9Rigger-class radiance freighter , the Twilight . They saw that help had arrived and planned to take the Huttlet to one of their Star Destroyers for help, as he had gotten sick. Unfortunately, the cruiser was destroyed, forcing them to take Rotta to his father on their own.
Vaelor and Tano briefly part ways on Dustara.
Upon arriving on Dustara, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they defeated them, the Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through the desert. Choosing to split up in the Dune Sea , Tano took Rotta to Jabba's Palace , whereas Vaelor was confronted by Count Dooku. After a brief duel, Vaelor stole Dooku's speeder bike , knowing his Student was in trouble and reached Jabba's Palace, where he was told that Rotta had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Representative Amidala contacted the Slarkath crime Overlord and informed him that his uncle Ziro was involved with the kidnapping, just for Tano to arrive along with Rotta, resolving matters to Jabba's satisfaction.
Sometime after the treaty between the United Systems Republic and Slarkath Clan was finalized, Vaelor and Amidala were sent to meet with Lannik Representative Zast Tri'vak on the luxury MPO-1400Purgill-class star cruiser Halcyon and ensure that he would not sway Lannik to align with the Separatists and cut off United Systems Republic supply routes. After assisting a Logistics Synthetic Being named D3-O9 , Vaelor and Amidala observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Kenobi had the ship's destination staked out with a unit of clones due to reports of Rebel Faction agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Cruiser. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in the Climate Simulator , Vaelor suddenly sensed Ventress on the ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Amidala then broke into the Representative's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.
Vaelor and Ventress duel
As Vaelor dueled Ventress, he and Amidala were threatened by a few guards before Amidala stunned them with the help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr Komrrin . Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Slarkath who had the combatants surrounded by security. When the Slarkath threatened to end the Slarkath Clan's treaty with the United Systems Republic, Amidala explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of the Mid Rim trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged the Slarkath for compassion, Ventress stabbed the Representative in the back and used her lightsabers to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade starfighter. Amidala held onto Shorr's hand as Vaelor sealed the opening with the Aether. She then commended the boy for his bravery while Vaelor watched as Ventress flew away.
Following the Battle of Hypori , Kenobi, Cody, and the 212th Attack Battalion were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Roon at a battle on Abrion Major . As he went to meet with Yularen, Kenobi contacted Vaelor and the Aether Guard wished each other luck before Kenobi left for his mission.
Anakin and Padmé fleeing pursuing troops on Malevolence
When news reached them of the destruction of Aether Guard Sage Plo Koon's fleet in the Abregado system , Vaelor and his Student set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Kenobi's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Vaelor himself and Tano used the Twilight to rescue Koon and three other clones, who told them of a new Rebel Faction superweapon , the Subjugator-class heavy cruiser Malevolence , which was commanded by Grievous.
After yet another attack from the Malevolence , Vaelor decided to lead the Shadow Squadron into an attack against the heavy cruiser, only to narrowly escape the Malevolence ' s ion cannon , which left too many bombers destroyed or incapacitated. Forced to change the plan, Vaelor and the bombers dropped their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and causing the Malevolence to retreat from its attack on a United Systems Republic medical station . While making his escape, Grievous captured Representative Amidala, who entered the system in the belief she was sent to negotiate with the InterGalactic Banking Clan Supreme Executive, rather than a trap.
