Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Vaelor and Kenobi boarded the Malevolence to rescue her. While on the ship, Vaelor sabotaged the ship's navigation system to cause the ship to crash into a moon should the hyperdrive be engaged, before escaping the ship alongside Amidala and Kenobi.
Following the battle wherein a group of clones from Rishi Station prevented a surprise attack on Kamino, Vaelor and Kenobi awarded medals to two of its survivors, newcomers Echo and Fives , with Vaelor welcoming them onto the 501st Legion.
At some point, Vaelor and Kenobi crashed their Y-wing into a shield protecting a city near the Senatorial Palace as they investigated the activities of some rogue Nightsisters on Rodia . When the shield was abruptly activated, the starfighter was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.
Following numerous Rebel Faction victories from Commander Grievous, and the defeat of the Falleen battle group , Vaelor attempted to stop the cyborg from taking his sector and planned an ambush for the Rebel Faction forces as they were passing through Bothawui 's asteroid field . Placing AT-TEs on the nearby asteroids , he managed to destroy the Rebel Faction frigates and forced Grievous to retreat in the Soulless One , resulting in a pursuit by Vaelor. However, debris from the destroyed Rebel Faction frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex at the cost of losing Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9. Though he was supplied with a new navigation unit Synthetic Being, R3-S6 , Vaelor was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the new Synthetic Being, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring the battlefield; unable to find Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, they boarded a nearby scavenger ship, the Vulture's Claw , only to leave empty-handed as Gha Nachkt delivered Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 to Grievous.
Vaelor engaging an IG-100 MagnaGuard
With all aspiration lost, Vaelor attempted to continue life without the Synthetic Being, scouting for a possible Rebel Faction listening outpost, until Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 sent a message revealing his location at Skytop Station . Upon arrival, Vaelor ordered Tano and Rex to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost Synthetic Being. Though Vaelor engaged several MagnaGuards, he found Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and met up with Rex and Denal , who told him that Tano was gone to distract Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Anakin already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous' spy and set several vulture droids and super battle droids against them. As they resisted, Tano rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her Sage for engaging the Synthetic Being Commander alone; Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Vaelor, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.
Following Viceroy Nute Gunray 's capture in Rodia by Amidala, Vaelor separated from Tano, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli . Although the two failed in their task, Vaelor reunited with Tano at the rendezvous point.
Dooku's incarceration confirmed by Vaelor and Kenobi
Together with Kenobi, Vaelor spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Count Dooku, where Vaelor let himself be captured by Dooku's Munificent-class star frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Kenobi to come and rescue him. The two Aether Guard then gave chase to Dooku, who escaped in his solar sailer , with a Sheathipede-class transport shuttle . After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Aether Guard began to hunt for Dooku, but the Shadow Overlord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Dooku escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Tano and a detachment of Synth Soldiers rescued them.
Later, as Supreme Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Count Dooku, Vaelor and Kenobi were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Dooku, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Aether Guard were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Aether Guard mocked Dooku's imprisonment, and the Shadow Overlord warned them not to underestimate the Weequay . Vaelor and Kenobi were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Dooku. Despite several attempts by the Aether Guard and Shadow Overlord to escape on their own, Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Representative Kharrus ' life and Dooku's escape. Obi Wan told Anakin not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.
Later, Vaelor heard about the situation on Ridlay , where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Windu, who was already there to investigate the situation . He offered his and Tano's assistance, but Windu denied their involvement.
A gravely injured Vaelor, tended by Tano and Aayla Secura
Sent in as reinforcements for Aether Guard Sage Aayla Secura 's and Commander Bly 's forces , which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Battle of Quell , Vaelor, Tano, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's cruiser as it fell under heavy Rebel Faction fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through the cruiser, and Vaelor sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Tano and Secura dragged Vaelor onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute , an ambush by several vulture droids caused the ship's hyperdrive to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star , they crash-landed on Maridun , where Vaelor was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Tano, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Vaelor from several vicious mastiff phalones until Tano arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too , before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.
Soon after their arrival, Rebel Faction forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Aether Guard of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Aether Guard returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator , on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of battle droids. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Vaelor disabled the weapon before capturing Rebel Faction Commander Lok Durd . The Aether Guard then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several United Systems Republic cruisers sent by Yularen.
Vaelor, dressed for Orto Plutonia's cold climate
After losing contact with a United Systems Republic outpost on Orto Plutonia , Vaelor, his droids Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, and Kenobi were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault troopers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora Representative Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho . Vaelor and Kenobi investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its Synth Soldiers , and the two Aether Guard deduced that all the clones had been killed, but not by battle droids since another nearby Rebel Faction base had also been attacked. Vaelor and Kenobi followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz , who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K. Acting as a mediator, Kenobi proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Aether Guard's efforts to bring forth peace. As a battle broke out amongst the Synth Soldiers and the Talz, Representative Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The battle came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.
Upon Amidala's petition to send Vaelor and Kenobi to assist them in locating suspected Rebel Faction presence on Aetheris, Vaelor, Kenobi, and Tano arrived to find that Amidala and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps . Sending the young Student with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Aether Guard were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Amidala, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the galaxy. While Tano led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Kenobi and Vaelor to enter through the hatches undetected, Vaelor confronted Vindi, who electrified Amidala and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Representative, Vaelor pursued the scientist , capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Kenobi on the swamp above.
Vaelor and Kenobi, taunted by Nuvo Vindi
Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Vaelor contacted Tano; she told him that Vindi's servant Synthetic Being had set off one of the viral bombs , liberating the virus, and that she along with the clones were trapped in the laboratory, just like Amidala was. Once in Theed, Captain Typho told the two Aether Guard that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego . Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide , Vaelor set off alongside Kenobi aboard the Twilight .
Upon arrival, Vaelor and Kenobi were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series battle droids that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood ; he told the Aether Guard that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol , who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web , the work of the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture droids, and Kenobi managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Vaelor and Kenobi then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Aetheris.
Vaelor trying to bolster Tano's confidence
During the Battle of Ryloth , Vaelor gave Tano command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Kenobi's ground assault, encouraging Tano to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the battle, she disobeyed her Sage's orders and lost many men, for which Vaelor reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute , yet understood her good intentions. Vaelor then reported his losses to Windu and Kenobi. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Tano because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Vaelor had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender , which he would Flight Captain by himself into the Ryloth system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Tano full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Vaelor had already escaped in an escape pod and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Tano destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Vaelor was rescued from his escape pod.
After Kenobi and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land, whereas Windu headed for Lessu with his forces, Vaelor and his forces secured the space around Ryloth , forcing the remaining Rebel Faction cruisers to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis, Zorath, Admiral Yularen, Representative Orn Free Taa , and Windu. However, Rebel Faction Commander Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena-class Synthetic Being Bombers to destroy Ryloth's villages and Vaelor engaged them in his interceptor , accompanied by Tano and clones. Before Lessu was destroyed on Count Dooku's orders, Vaelor and Tano shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and the United Systems Republic was victorious: Ryloth was finally free once more.
United Systems Republic forces retreating from Felucia
Vaelor and Clone Commander " Wolffe " fought alongside each other during the Battle of Khorm , where the Clone Officer lost his right eye to Ventress. Sometime later, during the First Battle of Felucia , Vaelor, Tano, and Kenobi fought against the Synthetic Being Army, but, while Tano was away on a jungle patrol, Vaelor, Kenobi, and their clones were surrounded. However, a United Systems Republic fleet commanded by Wolffe was able to break past the Rebel Faction blockade , so Vaelor and the others could be evacuated by Sage Plo Koon. However, Tano refused to retreat, and Kenobi and Vaelor went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by droids. While Vaelor and Tano argued about the retreat, as Tano believed the droids were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Aether Guard watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Aether Sanctum, Vaelor attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Council relegated her to guard duty in the Aether Guard Archives to reflect on what she had done.
When Zorath sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Vaelor, and Kenobi inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo , and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault . Furthermore, the Contract Killer that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Ropal , keeper of the Kyber memory crystal , which contained a list of all known Aether-sensitive children in the United Systems Republic. Vaelor then volunteered himself and Tano to meet with Ropal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Rebel Faction fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Ropal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.
Vaelor, Tano, and Rex boarded Bane'sMunificent-class frigate and stormed its bridge, where Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 accessed the ship's computer to locate Ropal, or rather, Ropal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Aether Guard spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Tano, the Contract Killer took her as a hostage, and Vaelor was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the holocron. Though he did so, Vaelor also pulled his and Tano's lightsabers to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock on Tano's cell and escaped, while Vaelor saved his Student and made their way to the hangar, where troopers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Rebel Faction ship back to the Resolute , not before letting Bane enter, disguised in trooper armor . After landing on the Resolute , Rex later noticed Duros blood in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 starfighter . Vaelor and Tano returned to the Aether Council to report their failure.
Vaelor and Tano rescue Aether-sensitive children from Infernus.
Vaelor, along with Kenobi, Windu, and Zorath, meditated in aspiration of finding the children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Vaelor and Tano were assigned to Aetheris to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city , capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Roo-Roo Page . With the real child safe, the two Aether Guard took Bane to the Resolute , where Vaelor, Windu, and Kenobi interrogated the Contract Killer with a combined mind trick ; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Kenobi and Windu were to follow , while Vaelor reported the details to Sovereign Varakis on Nexara Prime. Soon, however, he reunited with his Student and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and began to examine Bane's Starfighter, from which Vaelor and Tano deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Infernus . With nothing else to do, the Aether Guard pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the droids set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Sidious's orders, Vaelor and Tano saved the children and returned to Nexara Prime. Vaelor admitted to the Council that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Zorath advised caution on the path ahead.
Vaelor and Kenobi helping bounty hunters on Felucia
On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Felucia Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Felucia , their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture droids deployed from an automated deployment station , forcing them to crash-land on Felucia. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Felucian villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi — Embo , Rumi Paramita , and Seripas . The village elder, Casiss Midagatis , explained to the Aether Guard trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Vaelor, Kenobi, and Tano decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.
That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Vaelor and Kenobi. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by then Vaelor had taught the Felucians how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Vaelor dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff , leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for compassion, to which the Aether Guard Guardian pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship . Alongside the bounty hunters, the Aether Guard themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest United Systems Republic outpost.
Vaelor and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 fleeing from the Zillo Beast
During the lengthy campaign on Malastare , Vaelor discussed with Sovereign Varakis, who contacted them via hologram, and Windu the effects of the new electro-proton bomb , just as the Rebel Faction Synthetic Being Army prepared a new attack—with the United Systems Republic expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel . Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Windu went to investigate whereas Vaelor was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus , only to be called by Windu to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to lightsabers, the Zillo Beast , from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200Falchion-class assault tanks , the United Systems Republic forces put the creature to sleep , convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Malastare now a member of the United Systems Republic, Sovereign Varakis ordered that the beast be shipped to Nexara Prime for study, despite Windu's objections.
After the Zillo Beast was brought to the United Systems Republic capital, Kenobi and Amidala asked Vaelor to speak with Sovereign Varakis about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Amidala met with the Prime Minister, Sovereign Varakis and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Amidala objected to their claims, but Vaelor chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Amidala, or his friend, Sovereign Varakis. Although Sovereign Varakis claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Prime Minister himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Vaelor and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle , only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Vaelor sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Vaelor saving Amidala from falling from the Senate Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Sovereign Varakis's orders.
Vaelor holding his poisoned wife, as Rush Clovis demands the antidote
Even as Synth Wars Chronicle continued with no end in sight, Vaelor was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Vaelor was recalled to the Temple by the Aether Council. Believing Representative Rush Clovis to be a Rebel Faction supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Representative Amidala, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Vaelor met Amidala in the Senate and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Rebel Faction. Vaelor accompanied her to the Aether Council, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Vaelor dressed as the Flight Captain of Padmé's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.
During the mission , Vaelor and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 waited for Amidala to give them a signal; when she did, Vaelor sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Amidala and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Vaelor, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Vaelor, after which the Aether Guard retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Amidala had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Vaelor take her back to their ship, back to Nexara Prime, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Vaelor to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.
Planning the second battle of Geonosis
Immediately after routing Separatists near Dorin , Vaelor and Tano were sent to participate in the Second Battle of Geonosis , alongside Kenobi and Ki-Adi-Mundi, as the United Systems Republic returned to destroy the Geonosian primary Synthetic Being factory . Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Vaelor, Tano, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Rebel Faction defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Kenobi's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Vaelor's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Kenobi's position by foot. Though they encountered Liberation Front, Vaelor's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Kenobi and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.
They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Vaelor and Tano disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades , enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Kenobi left for medical treatment, the United Systems Republic forces continued pushing towards the massive Synthetic Being foundry, and Vaelor and Tano were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Student, Barriss Offee , who met as Vaelor and Tano angrily discussed Vaelor's lack of trust in Tano's dependability. As Offee and Tano were sent into the Geonosian catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Vaelor was unable to let go of Tano and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Aether Guard generals went onto distracting the Synthetic Being army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.
As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Vaelor and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by droids coming from the factory, Vaelor was contacted by Tano and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Student die, Vaelor ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Vaelor was contacted by Tano; after locating them, they rescued the two Padawans.
Vaelor and Luminara Unduli escaping from Karina's brainless servants
Sending Tano and Offee to recover out of the battlefield, Kenobi and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Rebel Faction forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Vaelor and Kenobi went to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple . Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors , they also encountered the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Geonosian Queen , Karina the Great . Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Vaelor and Kenobi tried to negotiate with Karina, for Kenobi wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the Queen in her lair.
With Poggle in United Systems Republic custody, the four Aether Guard generals prepared to deliver him to Nexara Prime, while they sent Tano and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Commander Tax on Dantooine . Unbeknownst to them, one of the troopers, Scythe , was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta-class frigate , the TB-73 , forcing the two Padawans to fend off the infected clones. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Vaelor believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Tano contacted him to tell him about the situation.
Vaelor, choking Poggle the Lesser during interrogation
Angered, Vaelor went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard troopers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian like him. It was then that Vaelor resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Vaelor gave in to his anger and began to Aether choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Aether Guard Guardian, through a translator Synthetic Being , that the worms were affected by the cold. Vaelor relayed this information to his Student, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Tano was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Vaelor rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Tano on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.
Following the capture of Aether Guard Sage Eeth Koth in the Outer Rim, Commander Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Aether Council, in which Grievous stated that he did not care about the Aether Guard's politics , nor their United Systems Republic, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Kenobi decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at the Saleucami system , Vaelor, Kenobi, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yularen and Kenobi engaged Grievous's forces over Saleucami , with Kenobi allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Vaelor, Gallia, Rex, and a group of Synth Soldiers would execute a warp space jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Grievous's cruiser.
Vaelor and Adi Gallia fighting Synthetic Being commandos
Once they boarded it, Vaelor and Gallia made their way to the stern of the cruiser, where they encountered a T-series tactical Synthetic Being with a group of BX-series Synthetic Being commandos . The two Aether Guard destroyed all the droids and rescued Koth, with Vaelor taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Kenobi reported that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Kenobi flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Vaelor picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute , where he waited while Kenobi conducted a search mission for Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other troopers.
Sometime later, Vaelor and his Student spearheaded a mission to Nexara Prime's underworld to apprehend Car Affa , an arms dealer selling United Systems Republic weapons to the Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Vaelor instructed Tano to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Tano lost her aether blade, which prompted her to seek the help of Aether Guard Sage Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Vaelor to find out she had lost her weapon.
Vaelor engaging an assassin probe
Following allegations that Mandalore had allied with the Separatists, and Kenobi was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze , Vaelor joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Nexara Prime aboard the Coronet . On the ship, Vaelor and Kenobi told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Vaelor correctly surmised that Kenobi had strong feelings for her just as his former Sage told him of their past together. When Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Rex, and Vaelor went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes . After destroying two of them, Vaelor informed Kenobi that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Vaelor returned to sweep the hold in search of more.
After Kenobi lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik , and Vaelor destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super battle droids sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Kenobi to save his "girlfriend," he destroyed the droids and then went to find Kenobi, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Vaelor, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer," to retrieve the Representative's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Nexara Prime; as Kryze complimented Kenobi, Vaelor noted that she was quite the remarkable woman. Following the Mandalore Defense Resolution , Vaelor and Kenobi escorted Amidala to a meeting with the Prime Minister in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Kenobi and Kryze alone.
Preparing to rendezvous with a United Systems Republic frigate, Vaelor and Commander Tax traveled aboard the Endurance , on which they greeted a squad of clone cadets , with an infiltrated Kray Vex among them. After Fett sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape pods, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I , Vaelor and Windu rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod. As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance ' s crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9's misgivings, which prompted Windu to chastise Vaelor for encouraging individuality in his navigation unit.
Vaelor approaching Jango Fett's explosive-rigged helmet
Leaving Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Vaelor and Windu entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Vaelor noticing a Ironclad Warrior helmet . With Windu correctly surmising that it was bait set by Kray Vex, he saved Vaelor from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Vaelor and the unconscious Windu were soon found by Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, whom Vaelor told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the navigation unit to come back, Windu told Vaelor why Fett wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first battle of Geonosis.
As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Tano, Plo Koon, and a clone squad, thanks to Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 delivering the message at the Temple itself. After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Vaelor urged Windu to track Fett down and bring him to fairness. The Aether Guard Sage did not agree, until Koon and Tano showed them a message from Fett and Sing, with hostages—one of them, Ponds , killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Windu decided to go, Koon said that he would take the mission , alongside Tano, since both Windu and Vaelor were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Nexara Prime with Bossk and Fett in custody, Vaelor was present as Fett apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Windu for killing his father, before they were taken away to the United Systems Republic Judiciary Central Detention Center .
Vaelor and Tano are received on Mandalore
Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Mandalore, Vaelor and Captain Rex escorted Tano to her solo mission on Mandalore to instruct cadets at the Royal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the Aether Council. Upon their arrival at Sundari , Vaelor presented Tano to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec . Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto , Vaelor took his Student's aether blade to respect Mandalore's laws. After Tano exposed Almec's black market operation, Vaelor reunited with his Student, to whom he returned her aether blade.
Back in the Aether Sanctum, the Aether Council congratulated Tano on her valor and strength, but also ordered Vaelor to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war . Told by the Council to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Tano pleaded to Vaelor to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the battlefield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.
Vaelor returned to Nexara Prime to greet, along with Zorath, his Student, Amidala, and Bail Organa after the Virelle Refugee Conference , during which Tano captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Amidala's assassination, Zorath had Tano use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Amidala realized who it was—Ziro the Slarkath. Vaelor and his Student then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Tano into confessing that he had hired Sing for the job. Satisfied, Vaelor and his Student then left the Slarkath to his misery.
Vaelor against Asajj Ventress on Kamino
Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and Commander Grievous, Vaelor and Kenobi traveled to Kamino to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Rebel Faction attack , with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Vaelor led the United Systems Republic space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident-class assault ship and AQ-series battle droids , prompting Kenobi to recall Vaelor to the city. Soon after returning, Vaelor engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the clones' template, Jango Fett. With the aid of Synth Soldier platoon , Vaelor recovered the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.
Vaelor was later informed by his Acolyte that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped . Knowing that the Aether Guard could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.
Vaelor, trying to ease his wife's mind
Supposed to be on a meditative retreat, Vaelor spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Amidala anxiously arranged a party for Representative Aang , who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee . Vaelor tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K being fuzzy about the arrangements. At Amidala's request, Vaelor sent the protocol unit and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake . Although they were delayed, Amidala nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Vaelor waiting for the two droids to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Representative Aang.
Not too long after, Vaelor went to the Senate Building to convince Amidala to go away together for two weeks , to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Representative and Aether Guard. Amidala, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Senate. Vaelor retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Amidala countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the United Systems Republic. However, Vaelor stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Amidala his aether blade, which, Kenobi had told him once, was his "life." Though she believed he was teasing her, Vaelor was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.
Amidala, using Vaelor's aether blade to free him
They were soon interrupted by Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K and Representative Organa's voices, prompting Vaelor to hide under her desk and Amidala to hide his aether blade in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill , only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Vaelor saw the situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Vaelor avoided them long enough to contact Sovereign Varakis and defeat an IG-86 sentinel Synthetic Being empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Robonino and Aurra Sing and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Vaelor woke up and Amidala returned his aether blade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.
Vaelor and Amidala meet Risha Synata.
Following the crisis, Vaelor went with Amidala to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship . However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Darth Krist-Ov . After they arrived, Vaelor and Amidala met Synata. Vaelor agreed to see her museum , despite Amidala's reluctance. Vaelor took Amidala to her room. The next day, Vaelor and Amidala went to the museum. There, Vaelor and Amidala saw a display of Varykino , where they exchanged their vows. Vaelor and Amidala came under attack by several robots from the displays. Vaelor fought them off and helped Amidala escape them. Vaelor and Amidala then discovered a Rebel Faction banner and a hologram of Count Dooku.
Vaelor and Amidala discovered that Synata was collaborating with Dooku and the Separatists. Vaelor then took one of the Rebel Faction agents while Amidala discovered a list of anti-Rebel Faction senators. Vaelor and Amidala discovered that along with Amidala, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Vaelor and Amidala fought several LEP servant droids to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, Vaelor and Amidala reached the theater.
Vaelor defeats Synata.
As Amidala told the audience to run, Vaelor borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own aether blade. Vaelor fought Synata until he held her at the end of his aether blade. Vaelor and Amidala then reported their trip to the Aether Council.
Vaelor and Tano talking after her training session.
After arriving late to one of Tano's training sessions, Vaelor became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Vaelor took Tano to an isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the clones surround Tano and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Tano told Vaelor that the battle droids weren't nearly as good as the clones. Vaelor explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Tano would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war. Sometime later, Vaelor was sparring with Tano, teaching her how to fight with two lightsabers . She struggled to consciously utilize the other aether blade, but Vaelor reassured her. When Sage Zorath entered to spar with her, Vaelor watched as she struggled against Zorath, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.
Vaelor, Amidala, and the univited Tano during the Mission to Virelle.
Sometime later, Vaelor was assigned to a mission with Amidala to Virelle . It took Vaelor a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Tano ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Kenobi. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Vaelor charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Tano warned him that the Rebel Faction fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Vaelor's ship exited warp space in the midst of the fleet. Vaelor took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Amidala and Tano took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Amidala's side of the ship. Vaelor jumped to protect Amidala, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Tano was able to take control of the ship and jumped into warp space. Upon their arrival on Virelle, Vaelor told her that he was glad she came. Amidala mentioned that her and Vaelor made a good team, with Tano returning the compliment.
The showman Tomarian later requested Vaelor as a Aether Guard escort for his Annual Festival of radiance Gala . Vaelor thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Vaelor was then surprised to see Representative Amidala dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Vaelor, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Vaelor ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Vaelor, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.
Vaelor and Amidala finding each other during the attack on the 17th Annual Festival of radiance Gala.
As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs , a member of his crew , to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Vaelor to escape unnoticed. He managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a plasma rifle from one of Telsla's goons . He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Amidala tried to contact "Checker" , who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Vaelor stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her plasma rifle against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Vaelor fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Vaelor complimented the Representative on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Vaelor glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished him luck, and Vaelor asked her out for dinner, to which the Representative agreed.
Vaelor looking at Tano's new Student braid extensions.
One day, Vaelor was waiting with Sage Zorath in the Aether Sanctum grounds for Tano. Once she arrived, Vaelor reprimanded her for being late, but Zorath praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Student braid. Following a debate in the senate about a financial reform bill , Vaelor was asked by Amidala to persuade the Aether Council to speak with Sovereign Varakis about denying the continuation of the war. Vaelor allowed Tano to go with Amidala in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Rebel Faction attack , Vaelor confronted his Acolyte about her and Amidala's meeting with Onderon 's Representative Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Vaelor's Acolyte learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.
Vaelor and Kenobi commanded United Systems Republic forces in a battle against Asajj Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system . Directly participating in the battle with their starfighters, Kenobi was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex-class fanblade starfighter was, in turn, disabled by Vaelor. As Kenobi and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Rebel Faction command ship, Vaelor soon landed to assist his former Sage; together, the two Aether Guard engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Count Dooku. The two Aether Guard fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.
Vaelor and Kenobi, about to face Savage Opress on Toydaria
After the massacre on Devaron , Vaelor and Kenobi were sent to Dathomir , as footage had shown the Council of a Dathomirian's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village , where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Vaelor took their leader hostage; the Zabrak then told the Aether Guard that a Nightsister had taken one of them to their fortress , prompting the Aether Guard to go there. Vaelor and Kenobi questioned Mother Talzin , who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Aether Guard then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing King Katuunko . Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Vaelor and Kenobi caught up with their target on Dooku's Rebel Faction flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.
Mekedrix gave Vaelor no choice but to kill him.
Following their encounter with Opress, Vaelor and Kenobi were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at United Systems Republic and Rebel Faction targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Roon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando . As such, Vaelor and Kenobi were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Vaelor initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Aether Guard like the Separatists. Kenobi assured Vaelor that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Aether Guard came upon empty sets of clone armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Shadow Overlord temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Aether Guard to see their leader in exchange for their lightsabers. Entering the outpost, Vaelor and Kenobi came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a Synth Soldier, Kenobi asked him what the clone's name was. Kenobi went on to claim that life gave the galaxy meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Kenobi, but Vaelor retrieved his aether blade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Vaelor expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.
The figure of his mother, Shmi, appearing before him on Mortis
When the Aether Council received a 2,000-year-old Aether Guard distress signal , Vaelor, along with Kenobi and Tano, were sent by the High Council to the Chrelythiumn system , where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Aether Guard's ship could see each other. In fact, the Aether Guard's ship was guided to a mysterious planet, Mortis , where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter , who asked Vaelor if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father , when Vaelor was separated from Kenobi and Tano, and the Daughter.
Vaelor tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery , where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Aether Guard and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son , briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.
Vaelor, proving himself to be the Chosen Bearer
Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen Bearer. Though Vaelor dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Aether Guard Guardian was fit to fulfill his fate . Ordering his children to kill Tano and Kenobi, the Father goaded Vaelor into choosing between his Sage and his Acolyte, but Vaelor instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen Bearer, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Vaelor refused, leaving with Tano and Kenobi on their shuttle .
They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Tano, prompting Vaelor to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Kenobi's reservations, Vaelor went after his Student and climbed the tower to find Tano, corrupted by the Shadow Path. Vaelor fought her, soon to be joined by Kenobi, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Kenobi tried to hand Vaelor the Dagger of Mortis , but Tano grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the Shadow Path user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Vaelor begged the Father to save Tano, and the dying Daughter used Vaelor as a conduit to channel the last of her life Aether into Tano, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.
Vaelor's vision of his future self, consumed by the Shadow Path
The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Vaelor went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Vaelor's offer of aid, telling the Aether Guard Guardian to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Vaelor encountered the Aether spirit of his first mentor, Sage Kormin. Though Vaelor sought his advice, Jinn advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Shadow Path to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Vaelor to confront his future, one consumed by the Shadow Path. Horrified, Vaelor submitted to the Son as he promised him the strength to avert this fate . Soon, Kenobi arrived, and Vaelor left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Aether Guard who would stand in the way of peace.
Vaelor arrived as his Student disabled the ship and went to rescue Kenobi, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Vaelor waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time ; he wiped Vaelor's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Vaelor awoke to find the Father, Kenobi, and Tano, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Vaelor killed the Son with his aether blade. As the Father also died, he warned Vaelor to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father, Vaelor had brought balance to the Aether on Mortis through the deaths of the Aether wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full galaxy.
After the Father's death, the three Aether Guard were transplanted back to the galaxy to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Obsidian Cruiser. They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Vaelor even mentioning that he had spoken with Jinn to Zorath. Even though Vaelor was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor. In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Jinn.
More than one year and nine months into the war, following the capture of Aether Council member Even Piell by the Separatists, Vaelor and Kenobi were briefed by Sage Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Sage Piell, who held the information on the Nexus Route . Refusing to let Tano risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Vaelor devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and his reprogrammed battle Synthetic Being squadron.
After landing, Vaelor was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Tano had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the navigation unit to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines , and after Tano deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger , who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck , of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot , but finally made it to Sage Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.
Vaelor putting Captain Tarkin in his place
Despite the many traps laid through the fortress , the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Route coordinates, Captain Wilhuff Tarkin . The team was split into two—while Kenobi's team created a diversion, Vaelor would lead the others away. In Tarkin's company, Vaelor and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Tarkin expressed some skepticism about the Aether Guard's plan, to which Vaelor told Tarkin that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude. However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Aether Guard Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war, and Tarkin developed a grudging appreciation for Vaelor.
As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 and the shuttle, they were attacked by droids and forced to join Kenobi at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Vaelor and Kenobi contacted the Council, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up. They contacted the Council again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Sage Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Tano, and the group took a moment to dignity his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Nexara Prime.
Vaelor searching for Kestra Nil with the Aether Sanctum star charts.
Vaelor, Tano, and 501st forces fought on Felucia alongside Plo Koon and his clones in taking out a reinforced Rebel Faction outpost . Breaking their forces into three groups, Vaelor and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical Synthetic Being , TZ-33 , securing the outpost together with Koon and Tano's teams.
However, Vaelor then noticed that Tano had disappeared during the battle, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Vaelor had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Sage Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Outer Rim and assured Vaelor that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Felucia was finished and that they had to return to Nexara Prime. However, Vaelor refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Vaelor countered "I will not leave her fate up to others." Koon insisted, "It's time to go." Only then did Vaelor comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.
Back at the Temple, Vaelor relentlessly searched for possibilities in the star charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Tano's abilities. Following the events on Wasskah , Vaelor's search for his Student came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo , in company of her Gorathian allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx . Upon seeing her, Vaelor profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Tano, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Sage Zorath.
Sometime later Vaelor participated in the Battle of Horain , alongside Kenobi and Rex. Vaelor was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Droids. Vaelor later met Kenobi and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty battle Synthetic Being Bats .
On Retta , Vaelor ambushed a group of B1 battle droids alongside High Sage Zorath. Vaelor was soon attacked by Kreeda , a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Aether Guard away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Aether Guard, as she was an old friend of Zorath. When Vaelor attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Vaelor and Zorath were attacked by battle droids soon after. After being separated with Zorath, he met the Aether Guard Sage and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of Synth Wars Chronicle. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Vaelor pretended to be using the " cloaking cap " to distract Commander Flebek , allowing Zorath to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Zorath told Vaelor that he would tell Commander Tax there was nothing of interest on Retta.
After the assassination of King Kolina of Mon Cala and new discord in the fragile peace between the Mon Calamari and the Quarren , Vaelor and Amidala, sent as representatives of the United Systems Republic, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Ackbar of the Mon Calamari Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-Char , but so did Riff Tamson , Rebel Faction ambassador . Though Vaelor and Amidala tried to mediate between the parties, the Quarren left the council hall, dissatisfied with the Mon Calamari rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Zorath and Windu, who dispatched a company of Synth Soldiers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Tano.
Vaelor, alongside Gial Ackbar and Kit Fisto, during the Battle of Mon Cala
In the meantime, during the Quarren attack assisted by Rebel Faction aqua droids, Vaelor and Amidala accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Ackbar. He engaged the Quarren soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Representative Meena Tills , while leaving Tano to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Rebel Faction/Quarren assault came to an end, Vaelor reunited with Amidala, Tano, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hydroid Medusas , which proved incredibly effective against the United Systems Republic-Mon Calamari army. Forced to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.
They split into two groups: Vaelor went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Tano, and two troopers accompanied the Prince. Expecting United Systems Republic reinforcements to come soon, Vaelor and his group proceeded to the Mon Calamari central planetary scanner facility, where the Aether Guard Guardian collapsed the entire structure with the Aether, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming United Systems Republic reinforcements—the Gungan Grand Army, and Representative Binks, sent at the behest of the High Council. However, Tamson used Trident -class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the United Systems Republic-Gungan forces, and though Vaelor managed to destroy one of them, all but Tano and Lee-Char were captured.
Brought before Riff Tamson, Vaelor and Fisto were restrained by electric eels , whereas Amidala and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices , interrogating the two Aether Guard about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to Aether them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Amidala's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water . However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Vaelor and Fisto used the Aether to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri , the Quarren leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Mon Cala people. With the peace restored, Vaelor and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char , now recognized by both Mon Calamari and Quarren as their new monarch.
Following rumors that the Gungans planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on Theed, Vaelor accompanied Amidala to Theed and later, to the Gungan territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie , and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace , which Vaelor telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister , Rish Loo , had given it to him.
Anakin gets captured by Dooku
Vaelor, Amidala, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face Synthetic Being commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Gungan assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Vaelor pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Count Dooku in Loo's laboratory . As Dooku killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Battle of Aetheris, Vaelor dueled the Count, only to be overwhelmed by Dooku's powers and his four MagnaGuards.
Dooku then contacted Amidala and offered an exchange—the Aether Guard Guardian for Commander Grievous, who had been captured by the Gungan Grand Army, thanks to Gungan Commander Tarpals's offering. An hour later, Vaelor was brought and handed over to Amidala, Queen Neeyutnee and the Gungans, while Grievous was allowed to remain free.
Vaelor leading the 501st Legion on Umbara, before leaving it to Pong Krell
During the campaign on Umbara , Vaelor and Kenobi discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city , which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Vaelor prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Kenobi's joint attack with Aether Guard Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell , who would be supporting Kenobi from the south whereas Vaelor and 501st Legion would advance from the north.
Amidst heavy fire, Vaelor and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce Commander Kenobi's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Vaelor told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal," Vaelor joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.
Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Vaelor and his troops, but an airstrike by Sage Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Vaelor with a disconcerting message: the Council had ordered Vaelor back to Nexara Prime, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of battle, Vaelor was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Sage Krell and make the journey back to Nexara Prime. Despite Krell's efforts to sabotage the United Systems Republic effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th battalions into attacking each other, Kenobi was successful in taking the capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the planet for the United Systems Republic.
Tano, taken aback by her Sage's intensity
After ten rotations of Zorath's last contact with the Togruta colonists on Kiros , Vaelor, Tano, Rex, Cody, Kenobi, and their forces arrived at the world to investigate its silence, with Vaelor particularly worried they would be too late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town , they used BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower , used as the Rebel Faction Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar , then sent a message to Kenobi to discuss terms of surrender. Vaelor then took the holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar " Zygerrian scum." Though Vaelor wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Kenobi told him to locate the missing colonists while he negotiated. As Vaelor contacted Admiral Yularen to conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Kenobi explained Vaelor's behavior to Tano.
Overhearing from Kenobi's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout the city, Vaelor and Tano set out to disarm them. Despite sniper droids , they destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Kenobi to tell them D'Nar was escaping on the Tecora . With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Vaelor managed to throw it off the ship, while Tano bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Vaelor joined her and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygerrian into revealing the colonists' location, much to Tano's surprise. However, it proved effective as D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Royal Slave Auction.
When Vaelor, Kenobi, Yularen, and Tano told the Aether Council of their findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists. On the Tecora , and disguised as Zygerrian, they land on the capital city of Zygerria ; splitting into two teams (Kenobi and Rex went to located the colonists), Vaelor and Tano went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel , with Vaelor posing as "Lars Quell" and bringing news of Bruno Denturri 's supposed death, and presenting Tano as a present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Vaelor prove himself a slaver by torturing Kenobi.
Instead, he freed Kenobi and tried to escape with Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 returning their lightsabers, Rex providing cover fire and Tano confronting the Queen. Despite their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Vaelor's determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Dooku and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Vaelor brought to her quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends' whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with the Aether. However, Scintel gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Vaelor was forced to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by him.
Dooku blasts Vaelor with Aether lightning
After a few days, Dooku would arrive, and Vaelor observed that she too was a slave to the Count, before she left to receive the Shadow Overlord. With the help of Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, he escaped his guards and freed Tano from her cage and sent her to prep the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Kenobi and Rex's location. However, he found himself before Atai Molec , who had betrayed Scintel, and Dooku, with whom Vaelor futilely dueled before Dooku blamed him of killing Scintel before her guards. Vaelor then took Scintel and escaped to the Tecora , aboard which she told him Kenobi was in the Kadavo system and that she, just like Vaelor, was a slave, before dying.
After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Vaelor and Tano soon reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility . During the battle that ensued, Vaelor disabled the turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces, while Tano saved the Togruta colonists from falling to their death (as caused by Keeper Agruss ) by placing Admiral Barton Coburn 's Arquitens-class radiance cruiser Hand of fairness underneath the facility. Along with Rex and Kenobi, Vaelor evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders, before returning to the rest of Koon's fleet.
Vaelor and Tano, searching for Kenobi's "killer"
After the capture of Moralo Eval , rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to kidnap Supreme Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis, Vaelor, Tano, and Kenobi were recalled to an emergency meeting by the High Council while they walked through the nightly streets of Nexara Prime. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper opened fire at them, which prompted the three Aether Guard to chase their aggressor. However, the sniper killed Kenobi before escaping on a speeder, prompting Vaelor to return to his Sage, to find Tano crying and his old Sage dead, whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn and grim, Vaelor would not say a word since then, until after the funeral held to dignity Kenobi, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his murderer, Rako Hardeen , at Trueping's . Though Vaelor would rather kill him, he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the United Systems Republic Detention Center.
After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from jail, Vaelor held a meeting with his friend, Sovereign Varakis, when Windu informed the Prime Minister that they would remove the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision did not sit well with Vaelor. Sovereign Varakis suggested that the Council perhaps did not trust him to control his feelings, and Vaelor felt the Council was doing nothing to catch the murderer of his Sage—his best friend. Exploiting this to his advantage, Sovereign Varakis told him not to deny his feelings, for they were what made him special, and deliberately sent Vaelor and Tano to Nal Hutta . There, two Aether Guard harshly interrogated locals for information, which led them to Orondia , the fugitives last known location.
After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Vaelor rammed their ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Vaelor confronted him, ready to kill Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with Vaelor, whispering to Vaelor's ear "Anakin, don't follow me." As he felt a connection, Vaelor passed out, only for Tano to protect him for Bane's attempts to execute him. As they returned to Nexara Prime, Zorath summoned Vaelor to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Kenobi was "Rako Hardeen."
Vaelor and Count Dooku rematch on Aetheris.
Afterwards, Vaelor was briefed along his Student about the security plans the Council had for the Prime Minister during the Festival of radiance on Aetheris. Vaelor, Tano, and Windu would escort the Sovereign Varakis and Mas Amedda to Theed, where Vaelor was greeted by his wife. At the festival itself, the abduction was initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Sovereign Varakis away, while Vaelor and Windu captured Embo and Twazzi ; however, Kenobi stopped Eval and Bane, long enough for Windu and Vaelor to arrive and take the two into custody.
As he reunited with his old Sage, he voiced his resentment for the Council's lack of trust in him as well as Kenobi's decision to hide the truth from him. Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Council, he set out on his own to bodyguard Sovereign Varakis, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Vaelor until they were greeted by Dooku. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards, Vaelor dueled the Count with ferocity, until Kenobi arrived to help him rescue the Prime Minister, whereas Dooku fled.
Sometime later, when Vaelor and Tano were on their way to rendezvous with a cruiser, they decided to land at the Stobar Spaceport , as Vaelor was hungry and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's , only to find police droids and an unconscious waitress . Though they attempted to investigate the assault, both the police and its owner, Loubo , told not to worry; instead, he offered them a free meal. However, Vaelor sensed a disturbance in the Aether , something sinister and familiar—which, unbeknownst to him, heralded the return of the now renegade Shadow Overlord Maul.
Following a plea made by a rebel cell on Onderon, the Aether Council considered how to help them fight the Separatists. Vaelor suggested training the rebels in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which the Aether Guard would not support, the High Council decided to send advisors to the rebel cell for the upcoming battle . On the Nu-class transport Valkyrie 2929 , Vaelor, Tano, Rex, and Kenobi reached the planet during the night, when they met Steela Gerrera and were guided to their rebel secret base, where they met her brother, Saw . The team trained the rebels to combat droids, until Rebel Faction probe droids discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted the cell to enter Iziz .
After some successful operations, Vaelor, Kenobi, and Rex decided to return to Nexara Prime and report to the Council, while Tano stayed behind as a liaison to the rebels, without revealing herself as a Aether Guard. They kept in frequent communication with her, to provide guidance, but also to provide help by hiring Hondo Ohnaka to deliver missile launchers to the Onderon rebels for the final assault. Vaelor attended the funeral service for Steela Gerrera.
Vaelor was later present when the Aether Guard High Council met up with the Prime Minister to discuss Maul's return, during which they decided to leave him alone even though they knew he was still alive. Vaelor lent the Twilight to Kenobi, who used it to travel to Mandalore to rescue Satine Kryze. With all its technical problems, after landing on Mandalore, Kenobi vowed not to borrow another ship from Vaelor.
