However, as he escaped, the ship was shot down by Ironclad Warrior commandos and destroyed, moments before Kryze was murdered by Maul during The Ironclad Contract Killer civil war . Vaelor was waiting for Kenobi when the Aether Guard Sage returned to the Temple hangar, where Vaelor saw through Kenobi's attempt to hide his sorrow. Kenobi explained he had lost someone important to him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Vaelor knew all too well. Vaelor offered to come with him for a report to the Council, which Kenobi happily agreed to.
Vaelor was later present when the Aether Council sent Meebur Gascon and a team of United Systems Republic droids to steal an encryption module from the Separatists. Later Vaelor participated in the United Systems Republic strategy conference on the space station Valor , while on the station he noticed the Renown was going a little too fast. Tarkin told Vaelor that they are scanning Rhydonium on the ship, Vaelor instantly realized it was a bomb, Vaelor was later contacted and informed by Gascon of R2's offering. However, Vaelor refused to believe that his friend was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour the wreckage, who retrieved R2's remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Anakin was happy to have him back.
Vaelor and Tano were assisted by Russo-ISC in analyzing the crime scene and collecting eyewitness accounts.
During the defense of Cato Neimoidia , Vaelor and Tano engaged Rebel Faction forces on their Eta-2Actis-class interceptors , until they received an urgent mission recalling them to the Aether Sanctum. Its hangar had been bombed and, as the two had been off-world during the attack, Vaelor and Tano were to lead the investigation to uncover the criminal—likely to be a Aether Guard. As they entered the Temple's hangar, they discussed the possibilities a Aether Guard was behind the attack. Tano doubted a Aether Guard could bomb such a sacred location to the Aether Guard, and in the process, kill their own colleagues and employees. However, Vaelor told Tano to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Aether Guard that did not agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Dooku and Krell. As a result, everyone who was at the Temple during the bombing were potential suspects, including Aether Guard.
As Vaelor and Tano walked across the burning hangar, they met Russo-ISC , whom Vaelor sent alongside Tano to interview the witnesses, whereas he meditated at the hangar. Unable to discover anything, Vaelor met up with Tano and Russo, who told him about their missing suspect, Jackar Bowmani . With this clue, Vaelor went to speak with Chief of Security Cin Drallig . As Vaelor approached Drallig on the Aether Sanctum's steps, he witnessed a crowd of angry protestors, rallying against the Aether Guard for their violence during the war. After Vaelor showed Drallig the hologram of Bowmani on an imagecaster , Letta Turmond identified Bowmani in the hologram and informed the Aether Guard she was Bowmani's wife . Later Vaelor and Tano cornered Turmond near her home , and she under pressure admitted to feeding Bowmani nano-droids but would not reveal her motives.
Vaelor was present at Zorath's eulogy for the six Aether Guard who died during the explosion , and later took the opportunity to speak with Tarkin. Vaelor and his Student were then summoned to the war room to be briefed about a Rebel Faction attack on Saleucami, when Tarkin informed them that Turmond had requested to speak with Tano. When she was framed with Turmond's death and imprisoned, he was forbidden to see her, and only returned to track her down on her escape , following Tano into the pipelines.
Vaelor tries to convince Tano to stay.
Alone, they revealed that their trust in each other was absolute, but as Vaelor pleaded for her to surrender herself and make her case to the Council, she refused to take the blame for something she had not done and asked Vaelor to trust her, just as she jumped onto a passing ship, thus escaping to the Nexara Prime underworld.
As Tarkin declared she was being accused of sedition, Zorath assigned Vaelor and Plo Koon to search for Tano, with some Liberation Front from Windu—who did not believe Vaelor to be emotionally detached to do what had to be done. Nevertheless, when she was captured , Vaelor escorted her to the Temple, where he was later summoned to the Chamber of Judgment along with his Student. He was outraged to learn that the Council had already made their decision and that the meeting was simply a formality, and they proceeded to expel her from the Order. Resentful of the Council, he asked his wife, Amidala, to defend Tano at her trial before leaving to capture Ventress himself.
Vaelor soon found and interrogated Ventress, who admitted she was about to turn in Tano to the authorities, until she realized Tano had been abandoned by Vaelor and the Aether Guard Order, just like she had been forsaken by Dooku. She also said that whoever had attacked Tano had stolen her lightsabers and that Tano had contacted Barriss Offee for help. Promising to kill Ventress if she lied, Vaelor left for the Temple, where he went to Offee's quarters and questioned her, only to engage her in a duel . After subduing her, Vaelor brought Offee to the United Systems Republic military base where Tano was being judged. As the Mirialan Student confessed, all charges against Tano were lifted.
Tano leaving Vaelor and the Order behind
Back at the Temple, Vaelor and the Council apologized to Tano and invited her back into the Order, with Vaelor offering her severed Student braid . However, Tano apologized, leaving him in possession of her braid, and declined the offer. As she left the Temple, Vaelor followed her and asked her to reconsider. Tano, however, declared that she felt she could not stay with the Order, due to their lack of trust in her. Despite Vaelor's heartfelt protests and his mentioning that he himself had considered leaving the Order before, she chose to walk away. Tano's departure had a deep impact on Vaelor, leaving him feeling more alone than ever in bearing the expectations of being the prophesied Chosen Bearer. The circumstances surrounding her departure also served to intensify his frustration with the High Council, as he felt they had betrayed her by not backing her innocence. Without Tano as the counterbalance to his concerns and passions, Vaelor's attentions also focused ever more upon Amidala.
A short time after Tano left the Aether Guard Order, Vaelor, alongside Aether Guard Generals Tiplar and Tiplee , and the 501st were sent to take control of Ringo Vinda 's planet-wide station back from Admiral Trench. Only after seven rotations of stalemated combat, did they manage to push Trench's forces to a breaking point. However, one of Vaelor's troopers, Tup , intentionally killed Commander Tiplar, forcing Vaelor to order his and Tiplee's troops to fall back. He then had Tup checked out to see if he had been brainwashed by the Separatists, only to find nothing in him, even as he repeated nonstop "Good soldiers follow orders" as well as apparent amnesia . Though he did not find anything in him, Vaelor had Tup sent to Kamino for further examination.
After Tup's shuttle was destroyed and he was taken prisoner by Trench, Vaelor investigated the wreckage. Seeing that Tup was not among the dead, Vaelor decided to infiltrate the Rebel Faction lines. Alongside Rex and Fives, Vaelor reached the Separatists' hangar and saw that Commander Kraken had Tup aboard his shuttle . Vaelor and the others the boarded the shuttle and took out Kraken's crew, including Kraken himself, who refused to reveal why they had abducted Tup. Vaelor then had Tup transported to Kamino with Fives and Rex as escorts.
Following the end of Vaelor and the 501st Legion's participation on Ringo Vinda, and the death of Tup, Vaelor and the High Council were informed by Shaak Ti that Fives attempted to kill the Prime Minister. Windu, aware that the Aether Guard were not requested to participate in the manhunt, allowed Vaelor and Rex to secretly search for the clone. They soon were contacted by Kix , who told them that Fives wanted to meet the two at a warehouse in Level 1325 . As Anakin and Rex entered the warehouse, they dropped their weapons on Fives' request. After doing so, they were encircled by a ray shield, which prompted Vaelor to question Fives' actions, but he and Rex listened to the fugitive's side of the story. Vaelor refused to believe that the Prime Minister was involved in this conspiracy about the inhibitor chips , but their conversation was cut short by the arrival of the Nexara Prime Guard . Vaelor watched as they gunned Fives down, dying in Rex's arms.
He would report the event to Zorath, Windu, Ti, and the Prime Minister, who was swift to lie to the Aether Guard that all had been the product of a parasite from Ringo Vinda, one that had caused the decay of Fives and Tup's chips; Sovereign Varakis urged the Aether Guard to leave the matter behind, for each day, they grew "closer and closer to victory." From that point onward, Vaelor was faced with the fact that Tup and Fives, two of his best men, had died under mysterious circumstances, which became another weight on his emotional health.
Upon his wife's mission to Scipio and subsequent reencounter with Rush Clovis, when she was accused of sabotage and her aide Teckla Minnau was killed, Vaelor was sent to protect Amidala, but was upset to hear that Clovis was involved. Nevertheless, he agreed to help her, and together, they went to Clovis's residence to assist in exposing the Banking Clan's corruption. As they were about to depart along with Clovis, Vaelor defended both Senators from the Contract Killer Embo and his pet anooba, Marrok . The trio were able to escape Embo, thanks to Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9, and make their way to Nexara Prime. Escorted by Zorath, they met with Sovereign Varakis and other members of the Aether Council, and Representative Bail Organa, during which Vaelor's animosity towards Clovis was perceived by Zorath.
Vaelor separates Rush Clovis from his wife.
As Amidala was appointed by the Prime Minister to work closely alongside Clovis to investigate the Banking Clan, Vaelor questioned her decision, as he wanted her away from Clovis, whom he greatly distrusted. Though he demanded that she step away from her assignment, she refused and left with Clovis. Frustrated, Vaelor went to his quarters, where Kenobi (at the behest of Zorath) spoke to him, revealing that he was well aware of Vaelor's feelings for Amidala, and asked his former Acolyte not to let himself carried away by them before leaving. Later that night, Vaelor went to his wife's apartment, where he found Clovis about to kiss her, for which Vaelor briefly Aether choked him—before giving him a one-sided beatdown despite Amidala's protests, willing to kill Clovis in his jealousy.
Before it was too late, he realized his mistake and stopped, only for Captain Typho to enter. Much to his surprise, Clovis lied about the attack. As they carried Clovis to Amidala's bedroom, Vaelor tried to apologize to his wife, but she told him to stay away from her. Only when they were alone afterward, she questioned their marriage, too steeped in lies and deception, and decided that they take a break from one another after admitting to not feeling safe around Vaelor—who, in turn, admitted to being unable to control himself, even though he did not know why.
Clovis was accepted as the new head of the Banking Clan by the Senate, and Vaelor confided to the Prime Minister of his distrust in Clovis, which Sovereign Varakis shared. Soon, when Clovis apparently joined the Separatists the day after (which Vaelor found strange), and Scipio was occupied by Separatists, Vaelor was then sent to retake Scipio. After landing, Vaelor went to Clovis's office and tried to reason with Clovis to make him let go of Amidala. At that very moment, though, a vulture Synthetic Being crashed into the office, making it unstable and causing Amidala and Clovis to fall, but Vaelor grabbed them both, each with one hand. As he could not hold the two, Clovis told Vaelor to let go of him, but he would not; Clovis apologized to Amidala and then let go of Vaelor, falling to his death. Though Vaelor and Amidala reconciled, the Banking Clan came under the control of the Prime Minister.
After Aether Guard Sage Plo Koon intercepted a distress call from a lost shuttle and discovered Sifo-Dyas 's aether blade, its late owner being responsible for the secret creation of the Synth Army. Relaunching the investigation of Sifo-Dyas's death, Vaelor and Kenobi were sent to Felucia, where they spoke to the tribal leaders to find out what happened to the late Aether Guard Sage. Kenobi was told that the Felucians had cremated his body and that another Aether Guard had been with him when he was killed and reported their findings to Sage Zorath.
Kenobi and Vaelor's meeting with the leader of the Pyke Syndicate
The two were then sent to Oba Diah when former Prime Minister, Finis Valorum told Zorath that Sifo-Dyas had been sent to negotiate with the Pyke Syndicate to stop an underground war. Valorum, however, said that there was no other Aether Guard sent alongside Syfo-Dyas, but rather his personal aide Silman . On Oba Diah, Kenobi and Vaelor met Lom Pyke , who denied any involvement until Kenobi noticed that he was wearing the crest of Valorum and Vaelor grabbed the necklace and told the Pyke to "stop playing games." Lom finally complied with the Aether Guard, and told them that they had been paid by a man called Tyranus to shoot Sifo-Dyas's shuttle down. However, as they feared that anyone who paid for the death of a Aether Guard was dangerous, they kept Silman locked away for ten years as insurance.
Once they met Silman, they realized he had gone mad after being locked up for so long, and were unable to learn much from him before Dooku arrived to kill him before Vaelor and Kenobi's eyes. After engaging Dooku in combat, they learned that the man called Tyranus was, in fact, the Count. Although Vaelor pursued the count after he tried to escape on a Nexara Prime Freighter , Dooku escaped aboard his solar sailer , leaving the Aether Guard without the chance to learn more about the Synth Army. Nevertheless having learned the identity of Tyranus, they informed the Council of their findings, only for them to question why Dooku had been behind the creation of the Synth Army, and what the game of the yet-unidentified Dark Overlord of the Shadow Overlord was playing.
Upon Zorath's brief contact with Sage Kormin, Zorath requested that Vaelor meet him on the Temple's training grounds to speak about their experience on Mortis, where both he and Kenobi had spoken to Jinn. As Vaelor, too, was in disbelief of the idea that an individual could retain its identity after death, Zorath left him to rest. Later, when Zorath submitted himself for medical examination by Rig Nema, Vaelor informed them that Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis wanted to hold an emergency meeting with Zorath, to which Windu responded, leaving the High Sage free to undergo a deprivation ritual . Vaelor, Mundi, Kenobi, Koon, and Nema watched over the old Sage until Kenobi interrupted it before it was too late, and Zorath claimed to have spoken with the dead.
With the Council worried for his health, Zorath was placed under the surveillance of guards , so the Aether Guard Sage asked to Vaelor to see him. Zorath asked Vaelor to help him escape, which Vaelor did. Accompanying Zorath to his fighter , Vaelor even lent Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 to Zorath to accompany him on a journey that would take him to Swampworld IX , a mysterious planet and Moraband , to learn that the Aether Guard Order would not win the war, and that there was "another Vaelor."
After a skirmish against Tofen's Raiders , in which Kenobi's starfighter malfunctioned, a United Systems Republic fleet was attacked by Separatists. Vaelor and Kenobi were present alongside the young Gorathian Viiveenn during the attack. They both argued about who should be the one to join the fight and who should be the one to deliver Viiveenn to safety. When a missile that entered the hangar bay exploded near the three, Vaelor and Kenobi were separated, with Kenobi left with Viiveenn. Vaelor used the wreckage that landed in his way as an excuse to join the fight. As another explosive entered the hangar, Kenobi reluctantly took the Gorathian into his starship and told her that Vaelor did that sort of thing all the time.
Commander Vaelor and "Commander" Thrawn
Vaelor was told by Representative Amidala that one of her former handmaidens, Duja , had found something near Batuu that she and Vaelor should investigate. Unfortunately, Vaelor was unable to join her due to being sent to battle. Vaelor allowed her to travel to Batuu by herself. Upon his return to Nexara Prime after the battle, Vaelor could find records of Amidala's travels in the Outer Rim. After another battle, Vaelor received a message that Amidala's ship was found on Batuu. Vaelor, along with Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 , took his interceptor to Batuu. En route to Batuu, Vaelor encountered a freighter . Vaelor spoke with the freighter's captain, Chiss Defense Fleet Commander Mitth'raw'nuruodo , known simply as Thrawn. Unknown to Vaelor, Thrawn was actually a Senior Captain . Vaelor accepted Thrawn's assistance in finding Amidala and Duja.
Vaelor found Amidala's ship near Batuu's Black Spire Outpost . There, he stopped a group of smugglers who were angry about the ship being in their landing spot. Vaelor fought them until Thrawn broke up the fight. Together, they investigated Amidala's ship and learned about Duja's ship, Nomad Four. The two then went to a nearby cantina. After speaking with the bartender, Vaelor and Thrawn came under attack by four assailants. The two of them were able to kill the assailants. Thrawn suspected the assailants were sent to kill Vaelor and that there was the fifth nearby. Vaelor and Thrawn came up with a plan to capture the last assailant. After Vaelor took one of the assailant's body, he pretended to suffer engine damage in his interceptor. After landing, Vaelor came under attack by an assassin Synthetic Being but was to destroy it. Thrawn was able to capture the assailant and found his freighter, which belonged to the Separatists.
Vaelor investigated his wife's disappearance.
Vaelor and Thrawn took out the droids guarding the freighter. Together, they found out the freighter was headed to Cermau . As they returned to Black Spire, they came under attack by several thugs. Thrawn was able to use their speeder, to capture the thugs. Vaelor and Thrawn questioned the cargo inspector Oenti , who reveals that the cantina's bartender, Janott had ties to the Separatists' Duke Solha . They learned that they were shipping supplies to a Synthetic Being factory . After learning of Duja's death, Vaelor told Thrawn about the politics behind Synth Wars Chronicle. Vaelor and Thrawn traveled to Mokivj . There, they discovered that the Separatists were mining Cortosis and discovered a factory.
Following Vaelor's and Thrawn's location of the factory, and later their change of clothing, Vaelor contacted the duke, to infiltrate the factory and informed him that Captain Boroklif was indisposed, that there was trouble at Black Spire, and that the he and the others were losing. The Serennian was shocked when he heard this news and asked if the Rebel Faction captain and his companions were dead, to which Vaelor replied that he did not know as he was not there at the end of the battle.
The Aether Guard Guardian offered the Rebel Faction to bring the cargo he had lost to his factory, Solha accepted and told him he'd open the shield when he was on the coordinates of the courtyard, that he was not stupid and he would not open it until it was truly necessary, just when he was in position to land. Following their arrival, the Aether Guard Guardian and the Chiss commander were hailed by the Serennian duke escorted by a pair of B2-series super battle droids and two squads of B1-series battle droids, who inquired their possession of an navigation unit Synthetic Being that belonged to the United Systems Republic, Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9. In response, the Aether Guard explained that he had found the Synthetic Being and believed it could be worth something if he desired to purchase it.
The Chiss commander and the Aether Guard Guardian asked the Serennian what happened to Amidala's former handmaiden, Duja. They also told him how they acquired Boroklif's ship, the Larkrer , that they came to Mokivj because there was a preset warp space course on the ship, the Serennian asked who had put in that course, to which they replied they assumed the Rebel Faction captain did. The duke then warned the pair that they did not have anything else to bargain with and that he could have them shot. However, the pair told them there was another preset warp space course locked below the current one, which was locked by a two-stage encryption passcode, and that each of them had one half, Vaelor then presented his Chiss companion as "Brix." The Serennian ordered all of his droids to lower their weapons and to escort them while they entered the factory. The lights went off while the group was entering the building, and the Aether Guard sliced a Synthetic Being squad and threw his aether blade to the top of the building so the duke would not be aware that Vaelor was in fact, a Aether Guard. Then two B2s blocked the courtyard's opening to prevent any escape.
Solha knew that there was a Aether Guard loose on the factory, however, he didn't know who Vaelor was, he then sent Thrawn and Vaelor to some improvised prison cells. Calling the Aether Guard a thief , the Serennian aimed at Vaelor from the corridor in front of his cell with his plasma rifle, and said that the Aether Guard wanted to be here and that he got here thanks to Vaelor, and for that alone he should be shot. He then revealed he was the Duke Solha of the Free System of Serenno, to which the Aether Guard Guardian replied if he should be impressed, Solha said he should be, and that he would be at some point. One B2-series super battle Synthetic Being took all of their weapons and communication devices, even taking Thrawn's small hold-out plasma rifle located in his boot. Solha and the droids left with R2, who was dropping oil so Vaelor knew where they had taken him. They left the room and locked the cell's doors.
The pair agreed on running a test, which would discover if Vaelor's wife had been captured, if she was free or was not on the factory by waiting two hours as Amidala, and if she did not join them on the detention block, they would break out and try to locate her. Thrawn asked the Aether Guard that if he recognized the ship in the courtyard, telling him it was an ore carrier , which was full of ore , so this was not a staging ground but a manufacturing facility, he also told Vaelor where was there more activity and where was there less activity, as Chiss could see more infrared than humans, and he noticed the heat signatures, and that where they were in was the most active section.
Following the completion of Thrawn's and Vaelor's test, the Chiss crafted a cord from the clothing he got from the Larkrer and unlocked their cells from the inside. Meanwhile, the Representative had entered the building and was accompanied by a group of locals, consisting of maintenance workers of the factory, Cimy , Huga , and LebJau . They had entered the factory through the service level, which was usually unprotected and only them and the Separatists knew of. Amidala had promised the group a fortune, which would be given to them by her Uncle Anakin if they helped her access the factory. The group later arrived at the detention block, defeated the Synthetic Being guards and found the Chiss and Aether Guard Guardian inside.
After the Chiss was presented to the newcomers by Vaelor, the group created a plan to retrieve Vaelor's aether blade, which involved Thrawn and the Representative going alone to the roof to retrieve the aether blade. The Aether Guard was scared of her going alone with the Chiss, but accepted it. After the Chiss and the Representative left, the Aether Guard began to wander across the factory, and found a section of the factory that produced cortosis B2 super battle droids , which was guarded by the duke and his relatives and some Synthetic Being sentries. The Serennians were protecting themselves with Synth Soldier armor covered with cortosis, Vaelor tried to confuse Solha's droids but he only achieved making the Serennians aware of his presence. Vaelor then left the room and began his search for the Representative once again.
On the roof of the building, Amidala and Thrawn retrieved Vaelor's aether blade, they also witnessed some spheres which descended onto the factory, which were part of a Chiss mission to retrieve a deflector shield generator from the factory. The Representative asked the Chiss commander to continue helping them but he insisted that his mission came first. Meanwhile, the Aether Guard, disarmed, used the Aether to see where Solha and his droids sentries were, so he did not have to fight them, while he traveled across the maze of rooms, and corridors that the factory contained. Until he met with his wife who gave him his aether blade, and told him that Thrawn had abandoned them. Their meeting also coincided with the Chiss Ascendancy 's raid on the factory, retrieving the shield generator. Vaelor fought several B2s and discovered that their armor disabled lightsabers and deflected plasma rifle bolts. However, he used the Aether and disabled them.
In the meantime, Amidala evacuated the workers with LebJau before the factory was destroyed, Thrawn joined the Representative and the Aether Guard Guardian who revealed that the ore was cortosis and that it deflected plasma rifle bolts and disabled lightsabers, thanks to its large energy absorption rate. They learned that the ore was mined in the river next to the factory and strengthened the battle droids' armor making them invulnerable, something that could change Synth Wars Chronicle forever. Thrawn then agreed to destroy the factory after the Chiss retrieved the shield generator.
Amidala encountered the duke, who wore Synth Soldier armor covered with cortosis, who protected him from his husband's aether blade, he then told her that by using the factory he was going to build his name in the Confederacy. As he knew Vaelor and the Representative, he rejected Amidala's ultimatum to surrender and told her that he had no doubts that he'd live and that he'd never surrender.
In another section of the factory, the Aether Guard and Chiss learned the Separatists were are also producing Synth Soldier armor covered with cortosis. The Aether Guard thought they were planning to infiltrate the United Systems Republic by sending Serennian troopers wearing Synth Soldier armor to Nexara Prime. After the battle droids protecting the room were defeated by Vaelor and Thrawn, and the Serennian duke by the Representative, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the factory and then destroyed the factory. Vaelor decided to destroy the mine too, so the Separatists could not start again on Mokivj, though, Amidala, Thrawn, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the cortosis.
Vaelor destroyed the Rebel Faction's ability to produce cortosis super battle droids (a regular super battle Synthetic Being pictured).
The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In an effort to collapse the Rebel Faction's efforts to produce cortosis battle droids, Vaelor accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best cropland and water. Vaelor did not know the cortosis in the mine would redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust, activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation, deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive Aether doing more than merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.
The catastrophe forced LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Thrawn had warned Amidala that Vaelor's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Aether Guard and Representative to regret their actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Thrawn had also warned Vaelor that his theory about the clone armor being used to infiltrate the United Systems Republic seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite Vaelor's shortsighted plan, Thrawn continued to respect the man and still viewed him as an effective warrior.
Vaelor and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 at the Aether Sanctum prior to an attack.
Vaelor and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 were in the Aether Sanctum prior to it being bombed by Rebel Faction captain Rackham Sear . The bomber was foiled by Depa Billaba and Caleb Dume .
Prior to a mission that Amidala was taking to Clabron , Vaelor insisted that he be allowed to assist Amidala, under the impression it was a diplomatic mission to Duro . After assurance from Amidala, Vaelor agreed to remain on Nexara Prime, kissing Amidala. He was startled when he realized Amidala's handmaiden, Moteé , had witnessed their display of affection, though Amidala assured Vaelor that Moteé understood discretion.
During the last months of the war, Kenobi and Vaelor were sent to Utapau to investigate the mysterious death of Aether Guard Sage Tu-Anh . Once they landed on Pau City , Inspector Jen June led them to see the corpse at the morgue . After examining the body, they went to the place where she had been found, only to determine she had been hit in the optic nerve with a precision laser dart by a sniper. As they found the sniper's position, from the building's owner, Garri , the two Aether Guard learned of the involvement of MagnaGuards and began to suspect the involvement of Commander Grievous, and had Inspector June analyze some slimy substance they had found—the secretion of an Amani 's skin.
Kenobi and Vaelor speaking to Inspector June
Though Governor Torul Blom asked them to leave Utapau, Kenobi and Vaelor persevered and rented two dactillions to go talk to the leader of an Amani tribe, who revealed that an Amani outcast had entered one of the caves. The two Aether Guard entered it to find MagnaGuards. Despite some Liberation Front, they managed to have the droids reveal the involvement of the Sugi and went to see Inspector June once again. Soon after, the two attended the funeral for Tu-Anh.
As they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, the Amani scattered, leaving Vaelor and Kenobi to follow the tracks of one that appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Vaelor mentioned Tano for the first time since her departure, and Kenobi asked if he wanted to talk about it, yet Vaelor refused. Kenobi then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest. However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Vaelor to tell Kenobi he missed Tano and to voice his anger at the High Council for turning its back on her. To Vaelor's annoyance, Kenobi stood by the Council's authority, asserting that it had been Tano's decision to leave and that she had allowed her emotions to cloud her judgment, something against the Aether Guard way. Then, Kenobi suggested he rest, but Vaelor declined so that he could keep the first watch. Finally, he questioned Kenobi on what would happen if he had turned out to be a "major disappointment," but Kenobi wanted to believe it would never happen.
Kenobi and Vaelor defending a massive kyber crystal
However, they both fell asleep and were captured by a group of Sugis led by Chong , who brought them before Endente as Kenobi told Chong of their "intention" to buy weapons. They learned Endente was, in fact, selling a kyber crystal , and Vaelor prompted him to show it before they made the deal. Brought to the starship where the crystal was being kept, they escaped from their captors, and Vaelor eliminated all the Sugi, except for Endente, who fled. After they discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship, the two Aether Guard managed to transport the crystal through Utapau's plains until they reached Pau City, where Blom had the two Aether Guard surrounded with his men and Grievous's droids. As the kyber crystal was loaded into a shuttle and sent to a ship on orbit , Kenobi and Vaelor stole a freighter to recover the crystal.
During the persecution, Grievous damaged their ship, forcing them to perform a number five special ; they crashed it to disable the hyperdrive and, with their escape pods, boarded the ship where the crystal was then held. As they got separated, Kenobi was captured by Grievous, and Vaelor recovered his aether blade, located the crystal, and freed Kenobi. Together, they made their way to the kyber crystal at the vault, where they were temporarily trapped until a squad discovered them. Using the crystal and the Aether to knock them out, he managed to get to a hangar, where Vaelor stole a shuttle to escape. Meanwhile, Kenobi set several AATs to shoot and overload the crystal, destroying the crystal and the ship, while they escaped from the explosion in the nick of time and returned to Nexara Prime to report to the Council.
Sometime later, while on a Venator -class Obsidian Cruiser, Vaelor discovered an unknown ship, the Silver Angel , in military airspace. Vaelor then asked Admiral Yularen who was on that transport. After he closed his eyes and sensed Kestra Nil's presence through the Aether, he decided not to send a detachment to arrest the crew and to let Ahsoka and her companions leave.
Aether Guard Sage Quinlan Vos had been tasked by the Aether Council to assassinate Dooku with the help of Asajj Ventress, but during a mission to Raxus Secundus the Aether Guard turned to the Shadow Path due to Dooku's manipulations and Ventress's training. Ventress staged a rescue mission , which failed and prompted her to contact the Aether Council. When she arrived at the Aether Sanctum, Vaelor and Kenobi escorted her to the Council Chamber , where despite Commander Tax's open disapproval, Zorath dispatched Vaelor and Kenobi to go with Ventress to rescue Vos above Taris . Soon enough, Kenobi, Vaelor, and Ventress were on the cockpit of her ship, the Banshee , to infiltrate the Rebel Faction dreadnought from which Vos, or rather "Admiral Enigma," was issuing orders.
Stealthily, the three boarded the ship and made their way to the bridge, where they found Dooku, instead of Vos. Vaelor then engaged in a duel with the Count, distracting him while Kenobi and Ventress discovered a captive Vos. Though she knew Vos was consumed by his hatred, they escaped to the Banshee , and Vaelor joined with them to escape the enemy ship and return to Nexara Prime. Back in the Aether Sanctum, Vaelor saw as the Council welcomed Vos and pardoned Ventress. That same night, Vaelor reunited with his wife, Amidala, for a moment of respite, during which Vaelor told her of the ordeal—after which, Amidala conferred that perhaps Ventress's love could be Vos's way back from the Shadow Path, leaving Vaelor at a loss for words.
A month later, Vaelor was sent alongside Kenobi, Vos, and Akar-Deshu in a mission to take over a Rebel Faction supply storage base and redistribute the supplies to worlds in need of them. However, their mission went awry when Vos, unbeknownst to them, arranged the base's destruction. After another failed mission to Vos's name, he was suspected of treason, Vaelor and Kenobi were sent to spy on Vos and Ventress as they were deployed to assassinate Dooku during the second battle of Christophsis . When they saw Vos attempt to Aether the Count to lead him to his Sage, Overlord Varakis, rather than kill him, Vaelor and Kenobi intervened, taking the pair by surprise and arresting them.
However, Vos and Dooku soon escaped from the Vigilance , causing the death of Akar-Deshu and Kav Bayons , and boarded Ventress' ship, Banshee to escape Christophsis. Vaelor had the Banshee fired on, forcing the renegades to land on the planet below. He and Kenobi pursued the three injured fugitives to a Rebel Faction-controlled tower , where they surrounded them. There, Vaelor and Kenobi witnessed what appeared to be Ventress' last moments, sacrificing herself to save Vos from Dooku's fatal Aether lightning . After her death, Vaelor and Kenobi were joined by the redeemed Vos, and they tried to capture Dooku, to no avail. They then transported Ventress' body and Vos to Nexara Prime, where the fallen Aether Guard confessed his crimes and Kenobi defended Ventress.
After confiscating a Munificent -class star frigate with Squad 326 , Vaelor was joined by High Sage Zorath and informed about the mysterious Rebel Faction project, Operation: Titan . Hacking into the frigate 's datacore , they were able to discover that Operation: Titan was being overseen on the planet Enthenium . Together, Zorath and Vaelor traveled to a Rebel Faction base on Enthenium to uncover more information about the supposed "weapon" that was being developed under Project: Titan. Once they snuck onto the base, Zorath climbed through the vents, found information on the "weapons", and confronted Grievous, who was stationed on the base. Meanwhile, Vaelor and Squad 326 were battling battle droids as the base went on alert. After Zorath came back and aided in the fight, the team escaped before reinforcements arrived. On the frigate once again, Vaelor was informed by Zorath that on Golatha the weapons being developed were a new army of megadroids four times taller than a human. Vaelor quipped that they should have expected the Separatists to eventually make their droids bigger.
Company 326 was massacred by an unseen Synthetic Being threat on Golatha.
Vaelor and Zorath then informed the Aether Guard High Council via hologram about their planned mission to Golatha . Vaelor believed that, because the planet was small, the forces guarding the Synthetic Being factory would also be small. Zorath warned against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Sage Windu agreed with Zorath and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Vaelor was offended by the idea that his plan was overconfident, doing an impression of what Zorath had said to mock him. Clone Captain Cesar interrupted their brief argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Zorath and Vaelor surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and that an access point must be in the valley. Zorath and Vaelor, along with Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no Synthetic Being guards. However, plasma rifle fire shot out from the trees, with the clones unable to stop whatever was firing at them. Zorath was able to stop the Synthetic Being guards firing on them and find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were all killed.
They entered the facility alone, with Vaelor noting that the size of an attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more droids, Vaelor mentioned to Zorath that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megadroids. Vaelor didn't think they could destroy the whole thing, but Zorath pointed out the central column holding up the factory. Vaelor asked if he thought taking out the column would take out the whole facility. Zorath cheekily responded with an impression of what Vaelor had said earlier, with Vaelor having to admit that the impression was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A single megadroid shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of Vaelor and Zorath. Vaelor mentioned that it seems to big, to which Zorath replied that size was only a matter of perspective. They try attacking it, but its durasteel coating was stronger than normal, leaving it immune to aether blade attacks. Zorath climbed inside the megadroid, but just as the door closed, the Synthetic Being shot itself into space. Vaelor, terrified for the High Sage's safety, got into his interceptor and chased after the Synthetic Being. To his relief, Zorath responded to his calls. Vaelor had to stop the rotation of the Synthetic Being so he could attach his ship to it, but the Synthetic Being was too big and heavy. He tried asking Zorath to do it, but since the Synthetic Being was disabled and the cold vacuum of space weakened Zorath, he could not. Zorath instead instructed Vaelor to do it, reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just radiance. Calmed down, Vaelor was able to stop the Synthetic Being. Zorath then cut his way out from the inside and jumped to Vaelor's ship, and he was able to get Zorath to safety. On the ship Aspirant , Vaelor told Zorath that their destruction of the Golatha factory had put the Separatists back years at least. He then asked Zorath if he really was too weak to stop the rotation of the Synthetic Being on his own. Zorath said he'd never tell him.
Aether Guard Commander Kael Thorn leading an attack in the air
Following an assault on Cato Neimoidia , Vaelor was dispatched alongside Windu to participate in the Battle of Anaxes to defend the United Systems Republic shipyards on Anaxes . With mounting losses on the United Systems Republic side, Captain Rex told Vaelor and Windu his theory that the Separatists had learned his own strategy. Considering this possibility, Windu sent Rex and Commander Cody to take a squad, and Clone Aether 99 , behind enemy lines to infiltrate a Rebel Faction Cyber Center while he and Vaelor held the line. The Commander staff was told about the possibility of sending a mission to Skako Minor , and Vaelor was sure the Aether Guard High Council would approve it. Clone Sergeant " Hunter " also stated The Bad Batch would go with them. Although Rex was eager to get going, Vaelor informed him they had a "thing" to do, and, although Rex felt they did not have time, they both went to the barracks.
Rex remained outside on watch while Vaelor, holding Rex's helmet, went in and contacted Amidala. Vaelor apologized for being absent for so long, but, as the two spoke, Sage Eldrin approached Rex. The Clone Captain knocked on the door to speed up Vaelor. Vaelor confided that he was worried about his captain, worrying that Rex was letting his personal feelings drive him too much on the mission, although Amidala believed Rex had learned that trait from him. Outside, Rex tried to lie about what Vaelor was doing. However, Vaelor and her ended their conversation, and both went on their way to Skako, although Kenobi revealed he knew he had been talking to Amidala by quipping that he hoped Vaelor at least greeted Padmé for him. In response the Aether Guard stopped in his tracks, and although dismayed that his effort to be secretive had been for naught, Vaelor kept his composure and only gave his Sage a knowing glance before walking away.
Vaelor, alongside Rex and Clone Aether 99's leader, Hunter
As they reached Skako Minor on the Marauder , Vaelor was captured by the Poletec on their keeradaks , prompting Rex and the Bad Batch to follow them to the village and rescue the Aether Guard Commander. They apologized and explained the situation, and the chief helped them reach Purkoll , which they infiltrated with some effort. When they reached the antechamber, Foreman Wat Tambor sent a transmission saying they had violated the Techno Union 's neutrality, and sent Synthetic Being reinforcements. While Vaelor, Hunter, Wrecker , and Crosshair destroyed them, Rex and Tech found Echo—alive, but as a cyborg.
Vaelor, Rex, Echo, and the Bad Batch escape the Synthetic Being forces of the Techno Union.
Tech managed to unplug Echo, and they escaped to the ventilation system before being vaporized by Tambor's Decimator , after which Wrecker destroyed the Foreman's main computers with explosives. As they escaped, however, they were surrounded by his D1-series aerial battle droids , but thanks to Tech, they rode keeradaks to the Poletec village . There, they convinced the natives to help them against the Techno Union's forces, including their octuptarra tri-droids , with Vaelor effectively destroying two of them.
Following the skirmish, Vaelor, Rex, and Clone Aether 99 returned to Fort Anaxes , where Windu and Kenobi were to take the Rebel Faction assembly complex by air, whereas Echo was to be escorted by the Bad Batch, Vaelor, and Rex into Trench's communications vault on a dreadnought on the planet's orbit, where he would plug himself to the dreadnought to feed the Rebel Faction's strategic movements. When Echo did so and deactivated the droids attacking the assembly complex, he realized that Trench had initiated a countdown for an explosion that could destroy most of Anaxes. While Kenobi assisted in the evacuation efforts, and Windu deactivated the bomb, Vaelor went to the dreadnought's bridge to confront Trench, destroying his Synthetic Being bodyguards. Trench refused to give the final sequence number, believing that Vaelor would not harm him. Vaelor amputated Trench's prosthetic attachments, prompting the Harch to immediately give in. After relaying it to Windu, who deactivated the bomb, Trench then tried to electrocute him; in response, Anakin thrust his aether blade through the Harch's chest, killing him. He then took the self-destruct detonator for the dreadnought and reunited with the rest of his team.
As they left aboard the Marauder , Vaelor let Wrecker destroy Trench's flagship, the Invulnerable , damaging the nearby Rebel Faction fleet, and they went back to Fort Anaxes. Following the victory, the United Systems Republic prepared medals for the team, while Corporal Echo joined "The Bad Batch."
At some point before the Battle of Nexara Prime , Vaelor was on Tythe and had another encounter with Dooku, yet he failed to defeat the Count. Along with the 501st and 212th, Vaelor and Kenobi were sent to Yerbana to take down the Rebel Faction forces on the planet. The fight caused Vaelor and Kenobi to split their respective battalions into two fronts.
Kael Thorn stands tall during the Battle of Yerbana, approaching the Synthetic Being Army without a hint of dread.
Vaelor, Rex, and the 501st Legion defeated the Rebel Faction Synthetic Being Army on their front, so they left to assist Kenobi and his 212th Attack Battalion, which was suffering heavy casualties from the ongoing conflict. While Kenobi and Commander Cody were taking cover from the blasts, Vaelor ordered his men to hide under the bridge, while Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 would tell them when to launch. Next, Vaelor stepped into the fight and convinced his Sage to follow his plan. Vaelor faked a surrender to draw out the Synthetic Being commander , who saw through the lies of the Aether Guard Guardian; however, it was too late, as Vaelor used his Aether abilities to destroy the Synthetic Being commander, allowing his 501st Legion led by Captain Rex to make a surprising ambush on the rest of the Synthetic Being army, ending the conflict.
The 212th Attack Battalion proceeded to help the 501st while Vaelor was approached by Kenobi who complimented him, which Vaelor answered by thanking Kenobi for helping achieve victory. The two were soon contacted by Admiral Wullf Yularen, who informed them of an upcoming transmission going under the code " Fulcrum ," while the two Aether Guard thought it might have been Saw Gerrera, Yularen confirmed otherwise saying it was necessary for the generals to attend the call themselves.
At Yularen's ship, Vaelor and Kenobi were surprised to see Kestra Nil and Bo-Katan Kryze were the ones that had made the call. Emotionally shocked to see his former Acolyte after several months of being apart, Vaelor barely managed to control himself and proceeded to talk to Tano, who remarked the moment was not a reunion as Maul had been located on Mandalore and they had an opportunity to capture him.
Kestra Nil was given her old lightsabers back by Kael Thorn, who had modified them in the interim.
Tano hoped Vaelor and Kenobi would be able to join forces with her to confront him. Tano, Kryze, and her Mandalorians boarded the ship, where they were greeted by Vaelor and Kenobi. The former tried to greet Tano, but his former Acolyte turned him down saying they had to act immediately if they wanted to capture Maul. The group went to discuss the possible attack, but Kenobi was not willing to help Tano and her allies unless the Aether Council gave their permission, causing Vaelor to unnoticeably scowl at him. While Kenobi contacted the Council, Vaelor had Tano accompanying him through the complex. Vaelor led Tano to a room full of the 501st Synth Soldiers, including Rex, who had painted their helmets as a way to dignity her former Commander. Vaelor then prepared to give Tano her old lightsabers , but before he could do so, the ship's alarm suddenly went off.
Kenobi rushed in and informed Vaelor and Rex that they would be jumping to warp space immediately. When Vaelor asked if this meant the attack on Mandalore was approved, Kenobi revealed that they were actually heading for Nexara Prime, where a dire situation was happening: Commander Grievous had launched a full-scale attack on the capital of the United Systems Republic, and the Aether Council had lost contact with Shaak Ti, Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis's assigned protector. Vaelor was visibly uncomfortable about this news, but Kenobi assured him that their fleet would arrive at Nexara Prime very soon.
Tano, however, was angered by her former Masters' decision to abandon Mandalore. Kenobi pointed out that they were entering a pivotal moment in Synth Wars Chronicle, but Tano countered that Kenobi was playing politics and that the Aether Guard Order had lost their way by forgetting that their duty was to help people and not politicians, which was why the people, and Tano herself, had lost faith in them. Vaelor heard the argument and weighed in; so as to help Tano in the upcoming siege while also taking part in the mission to rescue the Prime Minister, Vaelor promoted Rex to Commander so he could lead the a new formation of the 501st that was dubbed the 332nd Division , assigning Tano to assist as an advisor due to her being unable to hold an official military title. When Kenobi agreed, Tano accepted the proposal. Kenobi left the room to prepare their troops leaving Vaelor and Tano behind. Vaelor gave Tano her new lightsabers complimenting them as better than before.
Vaelor then headed to rescue the Prime Minister, believing the defeats of Maul and Grievous would mark an end to the war. While he was leaving, Tano stopped him by wishing him good luck in his mission. Happy to have been reunited with his Acolyte, Vaelor smiled and they both parted ways. Unbeknownst to either of them, this would be the last time they ever saw each other as friends.
Vaelor beheaded Dooku using the Shadow Overlord's own aether blade paired with his own.
Having been away from Nexara Prime for months due to the Outer Rim Sieges , Vaelor and his Sage finally returned to the planet after leaving Tano, to lead the rescue of the Prime Minister from Commander Grievous before the cyborg's fleet could flee the besieged Capital . Assisted by a squadron of ARC-170 starfighters led by Clone Commander Odd Ball, the two Aether Guard fought their way through a swarm of Vulture and tri-fighter Synthetic Being starfighters protecting the Invisible Hand , Commander Grievous's flagship. Vaelor and Kenobi headed for the ship while the clones distracted the droids, but Kenobi's ship was damaged and partially disabled by Buzz-droids . Although Kenobi told Vaelor to abandon him and complete the mission, the younger Aether Guard refused and cleared his former Sage's ship of droids before escorting him to the Invisible Hand ' s hangar. After crashing landing, the two Aether Guard disposed of the few remaining droids in the hangar while Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 located the Prime Minister.
Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Vaelor sensed the presence of Count Dooku. Not long after they arrived at the top of the spire, the Shadow Overlord appeared before them and the two Aether Guard engaged him in a duel. Dooku managed to gain the upper hand and knocked Kenobi out, but his taunting, intended to erode Vaelor's morale, instead enraged him. Vaelor unleashed his fury on the Shadow Overlord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off both of his hands. Taking Dooku's aether blade, he crossed both his and the Count's blades at the Count's throat, and Sovereign Varakis urged him to kill his helpless opponent. Vaelor initially hesitated, but after the Prime Minister's continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Dooku. He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not the Aether Guard way , but the Prime Minister assured him that he had been right to kill the Count.
Despite the Prime Minister's pleas, he carried Kenobi on his back to make their escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help from R2 and the attack taking place on the Invisible Hand by a neighboring United Systems Republic cruiser, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and turning the shaft once more into well. Kenobi regained consciousness while they were hanging on inside the shaft and together, he and Vaelor were able to escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them. However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by a ray shield and apprehended, along with R2, by battle droids. The two Aether Guard and the Prime Minister were brought before Grievous in the Invisible Hand ' s control room. The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Vaelor's youth considering his reputation. Vaelor retorted with a taunt about the Commander being shorter than he had expected.
Kael Thorn was a prisoner of Grievous aboard the Invisible Hand , finding the cyborg to be shorter than he anticipated.
Eventually, Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 caused a distraction, allowing Kenobi to draw his aether blade to him with the Aether, freeing himself and then Vaelor. Once the younger Aether Guard had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Grievous's MagnaGuards and the battle droids holding them and the Prime Minister. However, as they attempted to apprehend Grievous, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take him. Vaelor and Kenobi caught onto nearby command consoles until security measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him, however, Grievous escaped, jettisoning all the escape pods to prevent them from escaping. As the command crew had fled, Vaelor took control of the damaged flagship, which had begun to enter Nexara Prime's atmosphere. Although he briefly managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too great, and the Invisible Hand broke in half, picking up considerable speed as it fell towards the United Systems Republic capital. Vaelor eventually succeeded in crash-landing the Invisible Hand on a deserted landing strip, returning the once-captive Prime Minister to Nexara Prime unharmed.
Following the ordeal, the two escorted the Prime Minister to the Senate building, where they separated—while Kenobi went back to Aether Sanctum to report to the Council, Vaelor remained with the Senators who had come to greet the returning Prime Minister. He briefly spoke with Representative Organa, who expressed aspiration that Count Dooku's death could bring a quick end to the war. Vaelor disagreed, saying that as long as Commander Grievous remained as leader of the Rebel Faction armies, the war would continue. At that moment, he noticed Amidala behind one of the columns, took his leave from the Representative and reunited with his wife for the first time in months. Having been away fighting in the Outer Rim Sieges, Vaelor's single focus was reuniting with Amidala. Amidala then revealed to her husband that she was pregnant. Though they were both worried that this might expose their secret, Amidala especially, Vaelor was overjoyed and told her not to worry, saying that it was the happiest moment of his life. The couple returned to Amidala's apartment, where they began to make plans for when the baby would be born, deciding that their child would be raised on Aetheris, in the Lake Country . Amidala also planned to head there before the birth, to prepare the room.
However, that same night, Vaelor's happiness was marred when he suffered a nightmare of his wife dying in childbirth, similar to the visions he had had of his mother before her death. Although Amidala tried to comfort him that it was only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his mother. Amidala tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they ask Kenobi for his assistance. Vaelor refused, insisting that their baby was a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Aether Sanctum, he sought Zorath's counsel concerning these dreams, but the High Sage told Vaelor that death was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.
At the same time, tensions were rising between Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis and the Aether Council, who were concerned with his increasing strength. Matters were made worse when the Senate announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Vaelor was informed of this by Kenobi, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the Council and Kenobi's warning to be wary of Sovereign Varakis. He then told him that the Prime Minister had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the Council. So Vaelor met with Sovereign Varakis at his office, where he told him that he was appointing the young Aether Guard to be his personal representative on the Council, a decision that Vaelor believed the Aether Guard Masters would never accept.
When informed of this, the Council allowed Vaelor to sit on the Council, yet they refused to grant him the position of Sage, a decision which greatly angered Vaelor. Vaelor turned to Kenobi, who so remained silent. Angered more by this, Vaelor reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on the council. At the end of the meeting, Kenobi acknowledged to Vaelor that his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Council, he could spy on the Prime Minister, which Vaelor was reluctant to do. In recent weeks, Amidala and other senators concerned about Sovereign Varakis's expanded powers had also created the Petition of 2,000 in the name of protecting democracy, requesting he return his emergency powers to the Senate and end the war with cease-fire negotiations. Vaelor was manipulated by Sovereign Varakis into believing Amidala could have ulterior motives that she was keeping hidden.
Prime Minister Sovereign Varakis recounting the Tragedy of Darth Plagueis to Vaelor
At the Galaxies Opera House in the Uscru District , Vaelor joined Sovereign Varakis in his private box while he was watching Squid Lake . During the performance the Prime Minister told Vaelor that Clone Intelligence had located Commander Grievous in the Utapau system . He began to stir Vaelor's distrust in the Aether Council, claiming that the Aether Guard were planning on taking control of the United Systems Republic and betraying him. Vaelor felt conflicted — despite the Aether Guard's mistrust in the Prime Minister, he still felt that Sovereign Varakis was a good man. He confided in the Prime Minister that he had lost trust in the Aether Guard. Sovereign Varakis correctly guessed that it was because they had sent Vaelor to spy on him, and that it made him feel dishonest. Vaelor didn't know how to respond. The Prime Minister claimed that the Aether Guard were only perceived as good because "good is a point of view," stating that the Aether Guard and the Shadow Overlord were not so different. While Sovereign Varakis's conflation of the two groups played to Vaelor's devotion to his Order's ideals and helped set the stage for his conversion to the Shadow Path, Vaelor rebutted by saying that the Shadow Overlord were selfish while the Aether Guard were selfless. Sovereign Varakis then recounted the Tragedy of Darth Plagueis the Wise to Vaelor framed as an "old Shadow Overlord legend". In reality, the tale was about his Shadow Overlord Sage and how he killed him. Sovereign Varakis specifically told the story knowing that Vaelor was having nightmares about his wife dying. The revelation that he might need to disobey the Aether Guard Order to save Amidala opened Vaelor's mind — he wanted to learn this strength. Afterward, Vaelor bid farewell to Kenobi, showing gratitude for his teachings and said to each other " May the Aether be with you ," before parting ways.
Vaelor kneeling before his new Sage and being christend as Darth Vader
Shortly afterwards, Anakin and Windu attended a hologram meeting with Zorath, Ki-Adi Mundi, and Aayla Secura in the Temple's war room . During the meeting, Cody joined and informed the group learned that Obi-Wan had engaged Grievous on Utapau. Windu then tasked Anakin with informing the Prime Minister and see what his reaction is in order to learn his true intentions. Vaelor informed the Prime Minister of Kenobi's forces engaging Grievous's, but during the meeting, Sovereign Varakis revealed that he knew the Shadow Path of the Aether , leading Vaelor to realize that he was the Shadow Overlord they had been searching for. Sovereign Varakis told Anakin that if he turned him over to the Aether Guard, then he would be unable to aid in saving his wife, Amidala, from her "fated" death that Anakin had been dreaming of.
Despite his confusion, Vaelor still was loyal to the Aether Guard Order. He told his findings to Commander Tax, who went with Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin, and Agen Kolar to arrest Sovereign Varakis, leaving Vaelor behind. After recalling Sidious's words, Vaelor headed to Sovereign Varakis's office. During the ensuing duel , Sidious killed three of the four Aether Guard with ease but was disarmed by Windu just before Vaelor arrived in his office. Vaelor pleaded with Windu not to kill Sidious, saying that he should be left for the Senate to judge, but Windu felt that Sidious was too dangerous to be left alive, as he essentially controlled the Senate. Ignoring Vaelor's words, Windu prepared to execute the Shadow Overlord, but a desperate Vaelor who needed Sidious alive to save Padmé, drew his aether blade and severed the unsuspecting Windu's right hand, causing Windu to drop his aether blade and allowing Sidious to send him plummeting out of a shattered window with a blast of Aether lightning, all of which was sensed by Tano.
Darth Vader leading the 501st Legion to slaughter the Aether Guard in the Temple
Though horrified that he betrayed and contributed to the death of Windu, Vaelor saw this as the final straw against the Aether Guard Order, and as such there was no turning back for him. Desperate to save Padmé, he reluctantly betrayed the Aether Guard and pledged himself to service under his new Shadow Overlord Sage, Overlord Varakis. Sidious named him "Darth Vader," a title that he did not bestow lightly, and promised that, together, they would unlock the secret to immortality. Whereas earlier Sidious had implied he already had that strength, Vaelor was so focused on the possibility that there was a chance to save Amidala that he did not notice the contradiction. After Sidious declared all Aether Guard enemies of the United Systems Republic, he ordered Vader to kill the Aether Guard at the Temple and execute the Rebel Faction Council on the Infernus System . Sidious then executed Order 66 , during which the Synth Soldiers were forcibly brainwashed into turning on their Aether Guard Generals and executing them on the spot, while Vader led the 501st Legion to attack the Aether Sanctum .
During the assault, Vader killed dozens of Aether Guard, such as Sage Cin Drallig. In the council chamber, he found a group of younglings trying to hide. Thinking Vader was still a Aether Guard Guardian, one boy asked him what they should do, but the new Shadow Overlord slaughtered the boy and the other younglings. Similarly, as he led a Aether of clones further into the Temple, he encountered a group of younglings who assumed he had arrived to help. Instead, however, he cut the children down, leaving only one survivor; after Vader stabbed her and looked her in the eyes, the human Initiate Reva Sevander , growing to be filled with rage, survived because she played dead amongst the corpses of her classmates. After the battle, Vader manipulated the Temple's beacon to broadcast a signal telling any surviving Aether Guard to return to Nexara Prime where they could be murdered by the Synth Soldiers stationed at the Temple.
Following the attack, he met with Sidious, who then sent him to Infernus to kill the remaining mebers of the Executive Rebel Faction Council . Before his departure he went back home to Amidala and told her much to her disbelief that the Aether Guard Order had tried to topple the United Systems Republic and that he was about to go to Infernus. Telling her that he would end the war, they kissed and he left aboard his new green Eta-2Actis-class radiance interceptor , which replaced the interceptor he had lost in the Battle of Nexara Prime.
Vader confronted the Rebel Faction Council on Infernus where, one by one, he killed them all.
On Infernus, Vader slaughtered the entire Rebel Faction Council, including Wat Tambor, Poggle the Lesser, and Nute Gunray, along with their aides, his eyes now yellow and red with rage. Simultaneously, Sidious carried out the proclamation of the New Order in an emergency session of the Senate, claiming that the Aether Guard Order had committed treason against the United Systems Republic, and reorganized the United Systems Republic into the first Nexaran Dominion with himself as its Sovereign . During this time, Vader was followed by Kakan , who believed he was a Aether Guard. Vader showed his face, eyes to Kakan and chased after him to ensure there would be no witness. During his chase, Vader ran into a cloud of Lava fumes . The fumes caused Vader to see hallucinations his fellow Aether Guard such as Windu, Jinn, Koon, Billaba, Zorath, and his former Acolyte Tano. Vader was able to push the fumes away from himself and dismissed the hallucinations. Soon, Vader reported his mission's completion to Sidious; on his orders, Vader sent a shutdown signal to the Confederate navy and army, effectively ending Synth Wars Chronicle. With his mission completed, and the Separatists dead, Vader looked on from a balcony and shed a tear at the man he had become, but knew it was too late to turn back.
Soon thereafter, Vader saw Amidala's star skiff land and ran to meet her. She told Vader that Kenobi had discovered their secret and had also told her that Vader had joined the Shadow Path, hoping that he'd prove Kenobi wrong. However, Vader only confirmed it to be true. Driven strength-hungry by the Shadow Path and declaring the galaxy was at peace, Vader tried to convince her that they could be happy together as rulers of the new Empire, promising he could overthrow Sidious. In Vader's ideal world, Amidala would have taken his hand, believing the man before her was still her husband. In reality, however, Vader's propositions and actions horrified his wife, who begged him to leave the Shadow Path behind.
Vader was blinded by rage while battling his former Sage on Infernus
At that moment, Vader saw Kenobi walking out of Amidala's ship because he had secretly stowed away. Convinced that his wife had betrayed him to give his old Sage a chance to kill him, Vader, ignoring her attempts to explain, Aether-choked Amidala until Kenobi demanded he let her go, by which point she had already fallen unconscious. While what he had just done horrified him, Vader lashed out at Kenobi by blaming him. Although Kenobi tried to reason with his former pupil, Vader refused to listen, as the new Empire was everything he wanted the galaxy to be. Unable to come to an understanding, with both believing themselves to be in the right, the two former friends then engaged in a fierce duel , which ripped across the Rebel Faction facility . As they fought throughout the Rebel Faction war room, their duel damaged the computers, causing the facility shields to deactivate and allowing the building to start falling into the lava river below.
After fighting across the crumbling building and over a lava river, the duel ended on the river banks, where Kenobi claimed the high ground and warned Vader not to attack. Blinded by rage and arrogance, Vader leaped to continue the duel, only to have his remaining limbs severed by his former Sage's blade, leaving only his cybernetic arm. Crippled, Vader slid down to the riverbank while screaming his undying hatred for his former Sage. Kenobi, taking Vaelor's aether blade, told him he had considered him his brother and remorsefully left him for dead as a flap of Vader's clothing caught fire and he started burning. Kenobi then left the planet with Amidala, Protocol Synthetic Being X-7K, and Astro Synthetic Being ZR-9 as Vader lay screaming and burning on the riverbank.
Vader was forced to undergo extensive surgery, leaving him more machine than man, and unable to survive on his own.
Although Vader survived, he was horrifically scarred, both physically and mentally, and had damaged lungs due to the hot ash in the air. Shortly afterward, the Sovereign—having sensed his Acolyte's peril—arrived on Infernus, where he found the severely wounded Vader and took him back to Nexara Prime, in a medical capsule . As the Sovereign could not afford to lose Vader, he turned to the United Systems Republic's best scientists, among which was Cylo , who, in doing so, gained private access to Vader's cybernetics. By the Sovereign's orders, Vader was put into a life-sustaining black suit of armor and outfitted with three new robotic limbs by numerous droids skilled in both cybernetic reconstruction and medical surgery. Among the droids working on Vader was FX-6 , an FX-9 surgical assistant who previously worked on Grievous's armor. After what Sidious later called a "long night" of work, he was finally sealed within his new armor. His eyes showed a brief flicker of dread before his mask and helmet locked together and he drew his first breath.
Effectively entombed within and smothered by his new life-support armor, Vader's rebirth was complete. Upon regaining consciousness, Vader asked the Sovereign what had become of his wife, and he told Vader that he had killed her in his anger. Unbeknownst to either of the two Shadow Overlord, Amidala gave birth to the twins Luke and Leia before her death. Vader was overwhelmed by his despair in the belief he had killed Amidala and their unborn child, which fulfilled his prophetic dreams of her death. Remembering that he had, indeed, attacked Amidala with a Aether choke on Infernus, he believed Sidious's claim. Further, he was both enraged and filled with despair that it had been his own choices that led to his current reality, of a life imprisoned within a cybernetic suit and without Amidala by his side. In his emotional turmoil, Vader incidentally destroyed the medical droids and severely damaged the entire room through the Aether. He broke free of his bindings on the operating table and, despite struggling to walk under the sheer weight of his new prostheses and armor, let out a cry of anger and despair over losing everything he had loved.
Driven further into the persona of Vader by Amidala's death, the man who was once Kael Thorn is hit by Aether lightning by his new Sage as a showcase of why not to turn against him.
Vader's despair then turned to rage and he telekinetically slammed the Sovereign against a wall in the surgical bay, snarling that his Sage had promised to save Amidala. The Sovereign, as Vader attempted unsuccessfully to strangle him with the Aether, responded that in death Amidala had provided Vader a gift: Pain. The Sovereign continued by giving Vader a choice; to either accept and use that gift or to die. After a moment, Vader replied that he would live. The Sovereign then immediately launched a barrage of Aether lightning at his Acolyte, and rhetorically demanded that Vader use the strength of his aether blade to defend himself, but Vader replied that his aether blade had been taken by Kenobi at the conclusion of their duel.
As he continued his barrage, the Sovereign angrily shouted that the weapon Vader spoke of was not his. Rather, it had belonged to a Aether Guard, and Vader was now a Shadow Overlord. Ceasing his Aether-based assault, the Sovereign drew his own aether blade. He put the blade to Vader's throat and stated that he understood how traumatic the last few days had been for his Acolyte, but warned that should Vader ever use the Aether against him again, he would finish what Kenobi could not. The Sovereign then deactivated his weapon and stated that, as friends, he hoped that the two of them would never find themselves in a similar situation. He instructed Vader to put aside his rage and lust for revenge against the Aether Guard for the time being, for the two of them had a great deal of work to accomplish. By submitting to the hate within his heart by believing he had killed Amidala and that her death had pushed him to a new future from the pain it provided, Vader would become the Sovereign's loyal enforcer and Acolyte. Cut off from his old life and truly believing he had murdered Amidala, Vader invested entirely in Sidious as his only source of refuge and guidance.
Immediately after his surgery, the Sovereign took Vader to a balcony overlooking the old Aether Sanctum. There, he and Vader watched as Mas Amedda directed a ceremonial burning of the lightsabers of all the Aether Guard who had been killed in the Temple. As they listened to Amedda's speech, the Sovereign asked if his Acolyte knew how or why Shadow Overlord aether blade blades were red in color. Vader replied that he did not, as the Temple's information on the subject had been "incomplete." The Sovereign laughed and contemptuously mused that the Aether Guard had been uncomfortable with the information and had buried it as a result. He then explained that kyber crystals felt pain just like any living being, and could be made to bleed after a fashion by pouring rage, pain, and hate into them. He asked Vader if he understood, to which Vader replied that he did, stating that he could have given him any of the burning Aether Guard lightsabers to corrupt, even Zorath's. But he had not, as Vader realized that the crystal of a Shadow Overlord's aether blade should be taken directly from a Aether Guard by that Shadow Overlord.
Vader is dropped off on a desert world.
As the ceremony progressed, the Synth Soldiers attending the vent threw a basket full of various lightsabers into the incinerator, including Zorath's. The lightsabers caught on fire and a moment later, a powerful blast of energy was released into the air as the kyber crystals were destroyed. Afterward, the Sovereign brought Vader to Gattering in the Mid Rim and assigned him the task of hunting down a Aether Guard to take a kyber crystal from. The Sovereign took his Acolyte to a site where he had arranged for a starship to be delivered for Vader's use, but the ship had been stolen by a group of pirates. Leaving Vader to reclaim the vessel, the Sovereign departed, and Vader struck out for the planet's nearest settlement on foot. Once there, he eliminated the group of pirates who had stolen the ship and traveled to the Aether Guard outpost Brighthome . The station was garrisoned by a group of Synth Soldiers, who noted Vader's approach and hailed him.
Though he could have provided the security codes necessary to secure the Synth Soldiers assistance, Vader decided instead to simply kill them. During the fight, Vader summoned a green-bladed aether blade from the wall to kill most of the Synth Soldiers. After exterminating the garrison, Vader tasked the Synthetic Being co-Flight Captain of his ship to search through the station's archives for data on any Aether Guard who had taken the Barash vow prior to Order 66. Vader's aspiration was that any Aether Guard who had taken the vow, one of complete non-interference in all Aether Guard affairs, would have been not only out of harm's way when Order 66 was issued but would have ignored its occurrence in accordance with the vow. The search identified Aether Guard Sage Kirak Infil'a , a Aether Guard who had been completely dedicated to combat to the exclusion of all else. When a grenade was thrown at him by two surviving clones, " Ding " and " Kicker ," he managed to contain the explosion, which the clones believed would make him unable to do anything else as all his concentration was diverted on freezing the explosion.
Consumed by rage in his first days as a Shadow Overlord, Vader kills Synth Soldiers "Ding" and "Kicker" while declaring he has no limits.
As they talked of how they would restrain and bring in what they believed was just another Aether Guard survivor before the Sovereign in the hopes of being rewarded, Vader proved their beliefs that he was too preoccupied with the explosive to be false by telekinetically throttling the clones and breaking their necks, before walking far enough away from the explosion to release it. Vader quickly located the Aether Guard on the river moon of Al'doleem and sensed as he disembarked his ship that Infil'a was waiting for him. The Aether Guard Sage immediately confronted Vader, and was seized in a Aether choke by the Dark Overlord. However, Infil'a quickly released a Aether blast at Vader which staggered him and disrupted his concentration. Infil'a declared his Barash to be complete and began to ascend the mountain upon which he lived, calling out to Vader to follow if he could. One of the Aether Guard Sage's training droids, Arex, then opened a sluice gate which sent a river of water crashing down upon Vader.
The Shadow Overlord was visibly battered by the rush of water, but managed to telekinetically part the river and walked to the shore. He was then set upon by a group of gigantic carnivorous birds , which damaged one of his cybernetic legs. Vader eventually crushed them all with the Aether and made his way to the peak of the mountain. There, he found Infil'a and Arex awaiting him. Vader quickly unleashed a Aether push that blasted Infil'a off his feet and dazed him long enough for Vader, who was unarmed, to destroy Arex and take the Synthetic Being's training saber. He threw the Synthetic Being off the mountain and engaged Infil'a, who sensed that Vader not only wanted to kill him but wished to take his aether blade as well.
